Flow: once a booking's invoice reaches `PAID`, staff generate a single-use, 30-day-expiring **Rating Code** (`POST /bookings/{bookingId}/rating-code`, new `rating.issue` permission); the client later visits a public, session-less `/rate` page, enters their `bookingReference` + Rating Code (mirrors `confirmationCode` + last-name "find my booking," DR-016 -- Server Components calling `ratingsService` directly, no new public REST route), and rates the departure's actual assigned driver(s)/guide(s) individually plus the agency overall, 1-5 stars + optional comment. **New standalone `src/modules/ratings/` module** (mirrors `assignment`/`itinerary`/`immigration`) with three new tables: `RatingCode` (one per `Booking`, single-use via a nullable `usedAt` -- no separate status column, same "derived from a nullable timestamp" precedent as `Booking.holdExpiresAt`), `Review` (one per `Booking`, mandatory `overallRating`), `ReviewSubjectRating` (zero-or-more per `Review`, one row per rated driver/guide -- `guideUserId` points at `User` directly, mirroring `Assignment.guideUserId`, not at `GuideProfile`). Additive `averageRating`/`ratingCount` on `DriverProfile`/`GuideProfile` (removing DR-029/030's now-stale "no rating field" comments) and, new here, on `Organization` itself -- standing in for "the Tour Operator" in this single-tenant launch (DR-005). **Two new architectural precedents worth flagging explicitly**: (1) `fleetService.recordDriverRatingAggregate`/`recordGuideRatingAggregateByUserId` are this codebase's first no-ctx cross-module *writes* -- every prior "caller already gates" method (`listVehiclesByIds`, `getBookingForTraveler`, etc.) was a read; these are deliberately narrow (only ever touch the two rating columns, never a general update payload) and still routed through `withOrg`/RLS so a cross-tenant id fails closed at the DB layer even if the app-layer trust assumption is ever violated. (2) `ratingsRepository.recomputeOrganizationAggregate` writes `organizations` directly, the first time application code (not `seed.ts`) touches that table -- `Organization` has no owning module, same "shared, no owning module" precedent as `TaxRate` pre-DR-034. Averages are recomputed live via `AVG()`/`COUNT()` on every submission, not incrementally maintained -- simpler, and self-correcting if a downstream aggregate write is ever missed. "Paid in full" is checked via `Invoice.status === 'PAID'`, never `Booking.status` -- a booking can reach `CONFIRMED`/`COMPLETED` off a deposit-only payment (DR-027), so status alone can't answer it; new no-ctx `invoicingService.getInvoiceStatusForBooking` and `bookingService.getBookingForRating`/`repository.findByBookingReference`, plus `catalogService.getDepartureWindow` (deliberately skips `getPublicDepartureDetail`'s visibility gate, which would incorrectly 404 a `COMPLETED` booking's departure once it's no longer `SCHEDULED`), resolve the guest flow's eligibility end-to-end with no session. New permissions `rating.issue`/`rating.read` (not `booking.confirm` -- creates rows in the ratings module's own tables, matching the `itinerary.write` precedent instead), granted to `PLATFORM_ADMIN`/`TOUR_OPERATOR` by default; new `RolePermission` rows seeded live via `npm run db:seed`'s existing idempotent upsert loop (no code change needed there). **Per this session's explicit choice, the recommendation-engine change goes beyond the minimal ratings hook**: `assignmentService.recommendAssignment` (DR-029) already ranked vehicles and filtered-but-never-ranked drivers; this increment adds a new `compareByRating` sort (descending, unrated candidates sort last but are never excluded, per the spec's "may be deprioritized," not "excluded") for drivers, **and** introduces a brand-new eligible-guides list to the recommendation output for the first time (guides were never listed there at all before) -- `AssignmentRecommendation` gains `guides`/`recommendedGuideId` (keyed by `User.id`, matching `Assignment.guideUserId`'s convention). New `/staff/ratings` moderation/aggregate page (`rating.read`) and a "Generate Rating Code" panel on the existing booking-detail page (`rating.issue`, visible only once the invoice is `PAID`); new `RATING_CODE_ISSUED` notification event (EN/FR). New RLS policies + 3 cross-tenant test files for the new tables; new `tests/ratings.domain.test.ts` (pure rules) and `tests/ratings-lookup.test.ts` (DB-backed full guest-flow, mirrors `booking-lookup.test.ts`'s anti-enumeration/rate-limiting/single-use posture, seeded into the real primary org per that same precedent -- its `afterAll` explicitly recomputes the shared `Organization` row's rating aggregate after cleanup so the fixture's Review doesn't permanently pollute that live, singleton field). Schema pushed to the shared dev/production Neon database via `neondb_owner` (used ephemerally, never written to `.env`, same precedent as every prior schema change this session) with explicit user confirmation immediately beforehand. | V1, V4, V5, V6, V7, V8 |
| DR-038 | 2026-07-17 | Insights & Decision Making -- Module 10 of the same original 5-module spec (Ratings shipped as DR-037); the user picked this one next as "the easiest," confirmed by research before writing any code: **no new Prisma tables needed at all** -- every metric is composed live from data `booking`/`invoicing`/`assignment`/`fleet`/`ratings`/`visa` already produce. New standalone `src/modules/insights/` module with **no `repository.ts`** (owns no table, same shape as `notifications`) -- `domain.ts` holds the view types plus the few genuinely pure rules (`computeBookingsSummary`, `utilizationRatio`, `resolveBookingCountry`), `service.ts`'s `getDashboardSummary(ctx)` composes everything else through each module's existing public interface. New `insights.read` permission (`PLATFORM_ADMIN`/`TOUR_OPERATOR`, matching the `rating.read`/`itinerary.read` precedent) gates the page/route itself; every composed call keeps re-checking its own existing permission underneath (`assignment.write`, `invoice.read`, `fleet.read`, `rating.read`, `visa.process`, `catalog.read`, `booking.read`) -- confirmed both admin roles already hold all of them, so `insights.read` is a genuine additional gate, never a bypass. Two small, genuinely-missing additive methods elsewhere: `invoicingService.listAllForOrg` (staff-only via an explicit `isStaff` check, since `invoice.read` is also held by `TOURIST` for their own single invoice) and `assignmentService.listAllAssignments` (gated `assignment.write`, the existing manager-only permission, since `assignment.read` is also held by self-service roles who must not see the whole org). **Metric definitions are explicit design calls, not spec-literal, flagged for review rather than guessed at silently**: "active tours" = `Booking.status === 'IN_PROGRESS'` (the only "currently running" concept in this schema -- `DepartureStatus` has no such value); "conversion rate" = confirmed-or-further ÷ non-`DRAFT` total; "revenue"/"outstanding" = summed `Payment.amountMinor`/unpaid `Invoice` balance, **one bucket per currency, never combined** (confirmed zero cross-currency-summing precedent anywhere in this app, per BR-02 and the guest quiz's own "never rank by price across currencies" rule); "utilization" = a plain ratio (assigned-on-a-still-`SCHEDULED`-and-not-yet-ended departure ÷ total `ACTIVE` fleet), explicitly **not** a real scheduling-optimization/BI metric, same honest "simple, transparent" framing as `recommendAssignment`'s DR-029 disclaimer. **A real robustness fix surfaced during testing, not just a test workaround**: `getDashboardSummary`'s first draft composed its ~9 data sources via one `Promise.all` (plus nested per-package/per-assignment `Promise.all`/`allSettled` loops) -- this sandbox's Neon connection pool measurably choked on the resulting burst of concurrent `withOrg` transactions ("Unable to start a transaction in the given time"), reproducing even against an org with zero data, which pointed at concurrency itself rather than query cost. Rewritten to serialize every composed call and enrichment loop (sequential `await`, not `Promise.all`) -- a deliberate latency-for-robustness tradeoff appropriate for a low-traffic admin dashboard, and one that also protects the real production Neon pool, not just this sandbox. New `GET /insights` route (`insights.read`) + `/staff/insights` page (five sections matching the spec's own Bookings/Revenue/Operations/Customer Experience/Immigration grouping); new `tests/insights.domain.test.ts` (pure rules) + `tests/api/insights.api.test.ts` (DB-backed, a small deterministic fixture asserting concrete numbers) + `tests/api/insights.security.test.ts` (role-gate + cross-tenant). No RLS/schema change at all -- the first DR this session that didn't touch `prisma/schema.prisma`. | V1, V4, V6, V7, V8, V10 |
| DR-039 | 2026-07-17 | Financial Management -- Module 6 of the same original 5-module spec, picked next after Ratings (DR-037) and Insights (DR-038). Replaces `TourPackage.priceMinor` as a plain staff-typed number with a **cost-plus pricing engine**: staff configure six platform-wide, effective-dated rate tables (`StaffRate` [driver/guide/photographer/videographer per-day, per-country], `HotelRate` [per-country/room-category nightly], `TransportRate` [fuel/tolls/parking/vehicle-operating per-day], `FoodBeverageRate` [merges restaurant meals + drinks into one category enum], `ActivityFee` [freeform per-country attraction fees], `ImmigrationCostRate` [visa/processing/invitation-letter/border-permit per-country] -- all no `organizationId`/RLS, same precedent as `TaxRate`/`CountryRegulation`/`RolePermission`; "seasonal pricing" is just overlapping `validFrom`/`validTo` rows, no separate season concept needed), then build a per-package `PackageCostBreakdown` (nights/driver-days/guide-days/rooms/meals/transport-days/visa-requirement/`agencyMarginBp` + zero-or-more `PackageCostLineItem` rows for extra drinks/meals/activities) that computes `computedBaseCostMinor`/`computedSellingPriceMinor` and writes the per-seat result back onto `TourPackage.priceMinor` -- these two are genuinely org-scoped operational data, not shared config, and get standard RLS like every other tenant table. **`TourPackage.priceMinor` is now nullable**: a brand-new package starts unpriced until its cost breakdown computes a price (or an admin overrides one); `catalogService`'s `isBookable` treats `null` as not-bookable, and `bookingService.createHold` gained a defensive re-check (`effectiveUnitPrice` must be non-null) since TypeScript can't correlate that invariant across the two call sites. Existing (pre-DR-039) packages keep their already-set price, grandfathered -- no retroactive cost breakdown required. Per the spec's "Administrators may override calculated prices when necessary while maintaining an audit trail," `PackageCostBreakdown` carries `overridePriceMinor`/`overrideReason`/`overriddenByUserId`/`overriddenAt` (whether an override is *currently in effect*, not a history log -- the durable history lives in the append-only `audit_logs` table via `audit()`, same precedent as DR-025's visa-rejection reasoning). New `finance_config.read`/`finance_config.write` permissions -- deliberately **not** the pre-existing `finance.read` (that permission is about invoice/payment data and is held by `VEHICLE_OWNER` too, who has no business seeing internal rate configuration). `finance_config.write` is **never seeded to any role, including `PLATFORM_ADMIN`**, in `DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS` -- a new `financeService.isFinanceConfigWriter` check (`roles.includes('SUPERADMIN')`) blocks every other role unconditionally at the service layer, the same "route passes via `'*'`, service still rejects" layering `isCountryRegulationWriter` (DR-034) and the permission-matrix editor (DR-035) established; `PLATFORM_ADMIN` gets `finance_config.read` only. New standalone `src/modules/finance/` module (domain/repository/service/index, full DR-007 shape) with 12 new routes (`/api/v1/finance/rates/{staff,hotel,transport,food-beverage,activity,immigration-cost}(/[id])` + `/api/v1/catalog/packages/{packageId}/cost-breakdown`) and two new staff pages (`/staff/finance/rates`, `/staff/packages/{packageId}/cost-breakdown`). New RLS policies for `package_cost_breakdowns`/`package_cost_line_items` + matching cross-tenant test files; `tests/finance.domain.test.ts` (pure pricing-math rules) + `tests/api/finance-rates.api.test.ts`/`finance-rates.security.test.ts` + `tests/api/package-cost-breakdown.api.test.ts`. Schema pushed to the shared dev/production Neon database via `neondb_owner` (used ephemerally, never written to `.env`, same precedent as every prior schema change this session) with explicit user confirmation immediately beforehand; RLS applied the same way. Full finance test suite (31 tests across 6 files) verified green post-migration. | V1, V4, V5, V6, V7, V8 |
| DR-040 | 2026-07-17 | Fix: CI-only failure across `packages-v2`/`invoices`/`bookings`/`bookings-v2` API tests, discovered checking CI status right after DR-039's push. Root cause: `booking_reference_seq` (DR-027) and `package_reference_seq` (DR-028) -- the Postgres sequences `nextBookingReference`/`nextPackageReference` read via raw `nextval()` calls -- were created by hand directly against the shared dev/production Neon database when those DRs shipped, but **were never captured in any script or SQL file** (Prisma's schema DSL can't express a custom formatted default, so `prisma db push` was never going to create them either way). Every fresh Postgres -- CI's ephemeral service on every run, or any new environment bootstrapped via `npm run db:setup` -- was missing them entirely, so every booking/package creation call 500'd with `relation "booking_reference_seq" does not exist`. This had been silently broken on `main`'s CI for at least three consecutive pushes (DR-037, DR-038, DR-039) before anyone checked `gh run list` -- local dev/testing never caught it because it always points at the same already-provisioned Neon DB where the sequences already quietly existed. This is also a **real production disaster-recovery gap**: rebuilding the production database from schema alone would have hit the identical failure. Fixed with new `prisma/sequences.sql` (`CREATE SEQUENCE IF NOT EXISTS` -- idempotent, safe to re-run against the already-provisioned Neon DB without resetting its live counters -- plus explicit `GRANT USAGE, SELECT ... TO polco_app`, since the runtime app role only has DML grants, not DDL ownership, same as every other Neon-role gotcha this session) + `scripts/apply-sequences.mjs` (mirrors `apply-rls.mjs`'s comment-stripping/statement-splitting exactly) + new `db:sequences` script wired into `db:setup` (between `db:push` and `db:rls`) and both CI jobs' "Prepare database" steps. No schema.prisma change -- these sequences aren't Prisma-modeled objects. Re-running CI after this fix dropped the failure count from 10+ to exactly 1, confirming the sequence gap as the sole cause of the rest: `tests/api/visa-facilitator-queue.api.test.ts` still asserted `TOUR_OPERATOR` gets 403 from the visa queue, stale since DR-034 explicitly granted it `visa.process` -- masked entirely by the DR-040 bug in the interim (this CI job hadn't gone green since before DR-034 landed). Fixed by flipping the assertion to 200 (the correct, intentional behavior), not by touching `rbac.ts`. A second CI run then surfaced three more, distinct pre-existing bugs the sequence fix had been masking: (1) `tests/api/bookings.api.test.ts` -- this repo's first-ever API test, predating DR-027 -- still asserted the old `HELD` status literal (renamed to `AWAITING_DEPOSIT`) and called `confirm` directly on a still-`AWAITING_DEPOSIT` booking, a precondition DR-027 explicitly removed (`confirm` now requires `DEPOSIT_PAID`/`FULLY_PAID`); fixed the literal and set the precondition directly via `withOrg` rather than drive a full invoice/payment flow this file was never about. (2) `tests/api/invoices.api.test.ts`'s raw fixture `tx.booking.create` never set `status`, defaulting to the schema's `DRAFT` instead of the `AWAITING_DEPOSIT` a real hold produces, so `recordPaymentReceived` correctly refused the DRAFT-to-DEPOSIT_PAID/FULLY_PAID transition; fixed by setting `status: 'AWAITING_DEPOSIT'` explicitly in the fixture. (3) `tests/api/permissions.api.test.ts` expected 409 from PATCHing `role: 'SUPERADMIN'`, but `SetRolePermissionInput`'s `z.enum(EDITABLE_ROLES)` deliberately excludes `SUPERADMIN` as a syntactically valid value, so it 422s at validation before ever reaching `authService.setRolePermission`'s own (consequently unreachable via this route) `role === 'SUPERADMIN'` conflict check; fixed by asserting 422, the real and arguably more correct behavior, rather than changing the schema. All three predate DR-039/040 entirely and were simply never caught because this CI job hadn't gone green in a long time. Once `quality` went green, the `e2e` (Playwright) job -- gated behind `needs: quality`, so it hadn't run at all in several pushes -- surfaced 5 more pre-existing, unrelated failures. To investigate safely without touching the shared dev/production Neon database, a disposable local Postgres instance was initialized (`initdb` into a scratch directory, no sudo needed) and torn down after. Two were the same stale-literal class of bug already fixed above: `e2e/staff-dashboard.spec.ts` + its `booking-fixture.ts` helper asserted/needed the old `HELD` status (now `AWAITING_DEPOSIT`, DR-027). One was genuine e2e staleness from DR-033's explicit removal of the `/staff/departures` list-browse page (not an oversight -- browsing now happens via itinerary/booking-detail links): `e2e/departures.spec.ts`'s "browse from a package down to a departure" test targeted a page that no longer exists, removed; its redirect-check retargeted at `/staff/departures/[departureId]` (still real) with a placeholder id. One was a locator ambiguity from DR-027's new `bookingReference` sharing a CSS class with the existing `confirmationCode`: `e2e/guest-checkout.spec.ts`'s `span.font-mono` locator matched 2 elements once `bookingReference` started rendering nearby; scoped to `span.font-mono.font-semibold`, which only the confirmation code carries. **The fifth was a genuine, previously-never-verified bug in the e2e suite itself, not the product**: `e2e/fleet.spec.ts`'s driver-profile test did a bare `.click()` on the form's submit button immediately followed by an assertion on the pre-navigation page -- reproduced locally (network trace showed the redirect's POST request receiving `net::ERR_ABORTED`, and the driver profile was genuinely never created) -- root-caused as a race between the assertion running against the still-loading page and the in-flight server-action navigation, which Chromium cancels. Fixed by properly awaiting the navigation (`Promise.all([page.waitForURL(...), click()])`) rather than relying on Playwright's default auto-wait, which doesn't cover this case. `e2e/guest-checkout.spec.ts`'s passport-upload step could not be independently re-verified locally (needs a real `BLOB_READ_WRITE_TOKEN`, only provisioned in CI, OI-08) -- the locator fix stood up fine in the next CI run (progressed past it), which then surfaced one more real staleness bug in the SAME test, further downstream: after the `span.font-mono.font-semibold` fix, the final assertion still expected the `/find-booking/result` page's heading to equal the `confirmationCode` used for the lookup -- but that page's heading actually renders `booking.bookingReference` (DR-027), a different value from the lookup key. Fixed by also capturing `bookingReference` (the one remaining plain `span.font-mono` at that point in the flow) and asserting the heading against it instead. | V9, V10 |
| DR-041 | 2026-07-17 | Tracking -- the last of the two remaining modules from the original 5-module spec (Ratings/DR-037, Insights/DR-038, Finance/DR-039 already shipped). Combines two things confirmed explicitly with the user: real-time-ish fleet/vehicle location (building on DR-029's `StarlinkKit`, still staff-entered-only -- OI-09, no live GPS feed, unresolved) and booking/itinerary trip-progress ("where is this trip right now"). **No new Prisma tables/columns at all** -- same "compose via existing public interfaces, no repository.ts" shape as `insights` (DR-038). New standalone `src/modules/tracking/`: `domain.ts` holds two pure rule functions -- `resolveTripProgress(startDate, endDate, now)` (NOT_STARTED/IN_PROGRESS/COMPLETED + day-number/percent-complete) and `locationFreshness(lastLocationAt, now)` (FRESH/STALE/UNKNOWN, 24h threshold) -- plus `service.ts`'s single composing method `getFleetSnapshot(ctx)`. **Trip progress is deliberately computed at the Departure level, not itinerary-day level**: `Itinerary` is 1:1 with a single `Booking`, but a predefined-package `Departure` can serve several bookings, each with its own (or no) Itinerary -- there's no principled way to pick "the" canonical itinerary to represent a shared departure, so this stays a plain function of the departure's own start/end dates, confirmed correct via an independent design-review pass before writing code. **"Active trips" filters on `resolveTripProgress(...).status === 'IN_PROGRESS'`, deliberately NOT `insightsService`'s utilization-window definition** (`SCHEDULED` && `(endDate ?? startDate) >= now`) -- that window intentionally includes not-yet-started departures (correct for a utilization denominator, wrong for a page titled "Active Trips," where a future trip would confusingly show `NOT_STARTED`); caught by the same design-review pass. Composition follows `insightsService.getDashboardSummary`'s documented sequential-`await`-not-`Promise.all` discipline throughout (that sandbox connection-pool-exhaustion finding, DR-038, is the actual precedent -- not `/staff/schedule`, which turned out to still use `Promise.all`/`allSettled`, a design-review correction to the plan before implementation). New `tracking.read` permission (`PLATFORM_ADMIN`/`TOUR_OPERATOR`, mirrors `insights.read`/`rating.read` -- confirmed both already hold every underlying permission the composed calls need: `fleet.read`, `assignment.write` for `listAllAssignments`, `catalog.read`). Small additive widening: `fleetService.getStarlinkLocationsByVehicleIds` now also returns `lastLocationAt` (was silently dropping a field `fleetRepository` already had; only existing caller, `recommendAssignment`, ignores the extra field). New `GET /api/v1/tracking` route + `/staff/tracking` page (Fleet Locations table + Active Trips table with a day-X-of-Y/percent indicator) + `StaffNav` entry. Small self-service enrichment kept in scope (not split into a fast-follow, per design review): `/staff/schedule` (TOUR_GUIDE/DRIVER) gains a "Day X of Y" badge per assignment row via the same `resolveTripProgress`, no new permission. Explicitly deferred, flagged not guessed at: itinerary-day-level granularity (see above), a guest-facing "track my trip" page (staff-only scope, same posture as Insights), any new notification event like "trip started" (no clean trigger point exists -- `sweepLifecycle` is lazy/incidental on reads, not a dedicated job), and a real live GPS feed (OI-09, unchanged). New `tests/tracking.domain.test.ts` (pure rules) + `tests/api/tracking.api.test.ts` (DB-backed, asserts a not-yet-started departure is excluded from `activeTrips`) + `tests/api/tracking.security.test.ts` (role-gate + cross-tenant). No RLS/schema change -- the second DR this session (after DR-038) that didn't touch `prisma/schema.prisma`. | V1, V4, V6, V7, V8, V10 |

| DR-042 | 2026-07-17 | Settings -- closes DR-035's parked "Configure system settings" item, and is the last of the original 5-module spec (Ratings/DR-037, Insights/DR-038, Finance/DR-039, Tracking/DR-041 already shipped). Gives `TaxRate` (existed since Phase 0/DR-006, read-only via `src/lib/tax.ts`, no CRUD/UI until now) a real staff UI, and introduces a new **`PlatformRate`** table: the platform's own commission on every online payment ("the cost to maintain the platform," explicit user figure, seeded 5%) -- a single global rate, not per-country like `TaxRate`. Both are platform-wide, effective-dated reference data, no `organizationId`/RLS, same precedent as `CountryRegulation`/`RolePermission`. New standalone `src/modules/settings/` module (domain/repository/service/index, full DR-007 shape). New `Invoice.platformFeeMinor`/`platformFeeRateBp` (both nullable -- grandfathers pre-DR-042 invoices, same precedent as `TourPackage.priceMinor`, DR-039): `invoicingService.getOrCreateInvoiceForBooking` now also reads `getEffectivePlatformRate()` and stores the fee as an **informational split of the existing `totalMinor`, never added on top of it** -- `depositMinor`/`balanceMinor`/`totalMinor` are completely unaffected, and the field is deliberately staff-only on the booking-detail page (a guest could otherwise misread it as an extra charge). New `platform_settings.read`/`platform_settings.write` permissions; `platform_settings.write` is **never seeded to any role including `PLATFORM_ADMIN`** -- a new `settingsService.requireSettingsWriter` check (`roles.includes('SUPERADMIN')`) blocks every other role unconditionally at the service layer, the same "route passes via `'*'`, service still rejects" layering as `isFinanceConfigWriter`/`isCountryRegulationWriter`, verified by a security test that grants `PLATFORM_ADMIN` the permission directly via the live permission-matrix table and confirms the service layer still rejects it. New `GET/POST /api/v1/settings/tax-rates(/[id])` + `.../platform-rates(/[id])` routes and two new staff pages (`/staff/settings/tax-rates`, `/staff/settings/platform-rate`). **Navigation reorganized**: a new `SidebarShell` component (+ shared `settings-items.ts`) groups these two new pages together with five pre-existing tabs -- Country Regulations, Operational Rates, Insights, Users, Permissions -- behind one "Settings" entry in the top `StaffNav` (visible if the caller holds any of the underlying permissions); none of the five pre-existing pages' own URLs or permission gates changed, they're only visually nested now. New `tests/settings.domain.test.ts` (pure zod-input rules) + `tests/api/settings.api.test.ts` (DB-backed CRUD) + `tests/api/settings.security.test.ts` (role-gate, including the permission-matrix-bypass-attempt case above). Schema (`PlatformRate` + the two new `Invoice` columns) pushed to the shared dev/production Neon database with explicit user confirmation, same precedent as every prior schema change this session; no RLS change needed (no new tenant-scoped table). **Also added to the schema in the same migration, but deliberately left as an unused scaffold with no module/routes/UI/tests and no DR of its own yet, per explicit user instruction this session**: `SiteContent`/`FaqEntry` (intended for a future increment replacing the hardcoded guest-site About/FAQ content) -- flagged here so a future session doesn't mistake their presence in `schema.prisma` for a shipped feature; see the comment above each model in `prisma/schema.prisma` for the intended shape. | V1, V4, V5, V6, V7, V8 |

| DR-043 | 2026-07-17 | Password-management hardening -- closes two small gaps left over from DR-026/DR-035's password work. **(1) Every path that sets a password on someone else's behalf now unconditionally forces `mustChangePassword`**, not just `authService.createUser`/`resetPassword` (DR-026/DR-035, already did this): `scripts/set-staff-password.ts` (shell/DB-access CLI, used to recover Lam/superadmin after the DR-035-documented accidental `users`-table wipes, and for any credential set outside the app's own audit trail) now sets `mustChangePassword: true` unconditionally alongside `emailVerified: true`, instead of only conditionally touching `emailVerified`. `scripts/create-staff-user.ts` is deliberately left as the one exception -- it's the bootstrap-account path for a real, operator-chosen, permanent password (used once for `cyberpolco@gmail.com` per DR-026's own note), not a generated/relayed one; forcing a change there would contradict that explicit precedent. **(2) New voluntary self-service password change**, closing a real gap: `SUPERADMIN`'s own row is unreachable through `/staff/admin/users/{userId}` (`updateUser`/`resetPassword` both block self-edit, and the page itself 404s on `userId === ctx.userId`), so a `SUPERADMIN` who wants to change an already-real password they chose themselves (not a forced temporary one) previously had no in-app path at all. `/staff/change-password` (DR-026) was built only for the *forced* flow (`requireStaffContext`'s `mustChangePassword` redirect); it now also accepts a voluntary visit from any signed-in staff session via the same `requireAnyStaffSession` escape hatch (no permission gate, same as the forced case), taking a `forced` flag that only changes copy (temporary-password vs. current-password wording) and whether a "Cancel" escape exists -- the underlying `authClient.changePassword` call is identical either way, since better-auth verifies the current password server-side regardless of why the user is on the page. New "Change Password" entry in the Settings sidebar (`settings-items.ts`'s `SETTINGS_ITEMS`, no `permission` field -- `SidebarShell` now treats an omitted permission as "visible to any staff role") is the voluntary entry point -- deliberately grouped under Settings rather than a standalone top-nav link, since it's a self-service account action every role reaches the same way. No schema/permission/RLS change -- `mustChangePassword` and `requireAnyStaffSession` both already existed (DR-026). | V6, V9 |

| DR-044 | 2026-07-17 | Permission-matrix editor UX fixes -- two small changes to `/staff/admin/permissions` (DR-035), both explicit user requests. **(1) Column-header spacing**: the 7 `EDITABLE_ROLES` headers (some long, e.g. `VISA_FACILITATOR`) had no horizontal padding at all (the shared `Th`/`Table` components only ever applied vertical `py-2`), so they ran together with zero breathing room -- fixed locally in this page only (`whitespace-nowrap px-3`, now inside the new `PermissionMatrixForm`), not in the shared `Table` component, since every other table in the app relies on its current tight spacing. **(2) Batched save, reversing DR-035's original design**: every checkbox previously auto-submitted on click (`PermissionCheckbox`'s own `startTransition` calling a single-cell server action immediately) -- explicit user instruction changed this to buffer all 168 checkboxes' state client-side and only commit on an explicit "Save changes" button, with a "Discard changes" escape back to the last-saved state and a dirty-count label. New `permission-matrix-form.tsx` (replaces `permission-checkbox.tsx`, deleted) holds all matrix state in one Client Component instead of 168 independent ones. `actions.ts`'s single-cell `toggleRolePermissionAction` replaced with `saveRolePermissionChangesAction(changes[])`, which loops the exact same per-cell `authService.setRolePermission` call this always used (so every changed cell still gets its own audit row, unchanged) -- only the UI's commit point moved from onChange to a deliberate click. No change to `authService`/`rbac.ts`/the `GET`/`PATCH /permissions` API routes or their existing tests -- this is UI-layer only. | V6, V9 |

| DR-045 | 2026-07-17 | Booking code generation rewritten to an explicit, user-specified character-pattern spec, replacing two previously ad-hoc schemes. **Both** `Booking.confirmationCode` (was: 8 chars from a 32-char unambiguous-alphabet CSPRNG draw, DR-016) and `Booking.bookingReference` (was: `POL-{year}-{seq}`, sequence-backed, DR-027) now come from the same new `generateConfirmationCode()` (two independent calls per booking -- still two different codes, `domain.ts`). Spec: exactly 6 characters, uppercase A-Z + 0-9 only (36-char pool), no character repeated within a code, exactly 2 or 3 letters with every letter separated by at least one digit (no two letters adjacent) -- 10 valid letter-position layouts for 2 letters + 4 for 3 letters, 77,688,000 total valid codes. Implementation: `nonAdjacentPositionSets()` enumerates the 14 layouts programmatically (not hand-listed) so the adjacency invariant is provably correct; layout selection is **weighted, not uniform-across-all-14** -- a 3-letter layout represents ~3.43x more of the valid-code space than a 2-letter one (`P(26,3)*P(10,3) = 11,232,000` vs `P(26,2)*P(10,4) = 3,276,000` combinations per layout), so picking uniformly across all 14 (or a flat 50/50 between letter counts) measurably skews the result toward 2-letter codes -- confirmed empirically (a 50k-sample run came out ~72%/28% before the 35:48 weighting fix, ~42%/58% after, matching the combinatorial math). Character selection uses a Fisher-Yates shuffle over rejection-sampled `crypto.getRandomValues()` bytes (not a plain `byte % alphabet.length`, which would be measurably biased for non-power-of-two alphabet sizes 26/10 -- unlike the old 32-char alphabet, which divided 256 evenly). **"Never generate the same code twice" is enforced at two layers, not assumed from keyspace size alone**: the DB's existing `@unique` constraint on both columns is what actually guarantees no duplicate is ever persisted; new `bookingRepository.createBookingWithUniqueCodes()` wraps both booking-creation call sites (`createHold`, `createTailorMadeRequest`) in a retry loop (max 5 attempts) that regenerates both codes and retries on a Prisma `P2002` violation, so a collision degrades to an invisible retry instead of a failed request -- this exists for correctness (birthday-paradox math means a 50%-collision-chance point is reached around ~10,400 bookings at this keyspace size, not astronomically far off), not because collisions are expected day-to-day. Removed: `formatBookingReference()`, `nextBookingReference()` (the `SELECT nextval('booking_reference_seq')` call), and the old unambiguous-alphabet constant. **`booking_reference_seq` (DR-027/DR-040) is now dead**: still created by `prisma/sequences.sql`/`db:setup`/CI (harmless, left as-is rather than dropping it from the shared Neon DB unprompted), but no longer read by any app code -- don't mistake its continued existence for still backing `bookingReference`. Updated `tests/booking.domain.test.ts` (removed `formatBookingReference` tests, added generator invariant tests: length/charset, no-repeat, letter-count, non-adjacency, run 200x each) and `tests/api/bookings-v2.api.test.ts` (regex assertion updated from `POL-\d{4}-\d+` to `[A-Z0-9]{6}`). No schema/RLS change -- both columns were already plain `String @unique` with no length constraint. `lint`/`typecheck`/`build` all green; `tests/booking.domain.test.ts` (42 tests) green. | V1, V6 |

| DR-046 | 2026-07-18 | Merged the guest site's two "help me plan something" entry points -- the "tailor my trip" quiz (`/quiz` → `/quiz/results`, scored/ranked existing `TourPackage`s against country/trip-length/tag/site preferences, DR-016/024) and the separate "Custom trip" tailor-made form (`/tailor-made`, always created a real `TAILOR_MADE` `Booking` for staff to price) -- into one entry point, per explicit user direction. **Always bespoke**: package-matching/scoring is gone entirely (`scorePackagesForQuiz`/`QuizAnswers`/`TRIP_LENGTH_RANGES`/`catalogService.getQuizResults` all removed) -- every submission through the new `/plan-my-trip` now creates a `TAILOR_MADE` booking, same underlying `bookingService.createTailorMadeRequest` operation the old tailor-made form used, unchanged. The quiz's richer preference questions (country/tags/sites) are kept, but repurposed as **structured staff context** rather than a ranking input -- explicit user choice over dropping them or folding them into free text. New `Booking.preferredTags PackageTag[] @default([])` / `preferredSites String[] @default([])` (no RLS change, existing already-RLS'd table; `PACKAGE_TAGS` exported from `catalog/index.ts` so `booking/domain.ts` validates against the same vocabulary rather than hand-duplicating it -- the one deliberate exception to every domain.ts's "no cross-module imports" purity, documented inline). The "trip length" bucket question is dropped -- the merged form already collects exact travel dates, making a coarse length bucket redundant; the quiz's country question and the tailor-made form's country question were literally the same question asked twice, now one required field (required, not optional -- a real priced booking needs a destination, unlike the quiz's "No preference" option). New `src/app/(guest)/plan-my-trip/{page.tsx,plan-my-trip-form.tsx,actions.ts}` (closely modeled on the deleted `tailor-made/` files -- same anonymous-session-then-server-action shape -- plus the deleted quiz's `SelectableCard`/`DESTINATION_SITES` markup); deliberately no `StepIndicator`, same precedent the old tailor-made page already established (a bespoke request has no departure to pick, so it isn't part of that numbered wizard). `BOOKING_WIZARD_STEPS` shrunk from 7 steps to 5 (dropped `'Tailor my trip'`/`'Matches'`, which belonged only to the deleted quiz) -- confirmed by reading `booking/[bookingId]/page.tsx` directly that a fresh booking of *either* origin already skips straight to the Travelers/Passport/Add-ons sequence, so the merged flow needs no step of its own; every remaining `currentIndex` literal across `book/[departureId]`, `booking/[bookingId]/{travelers/new,passport,addons,page}.tsx` re-indexed by -2. `next.config.mjs` gained its first `redirects()` block: `/quiz`, `/quiz/results`, `/tailor-made` → `/plan-my-trip` (permanent), so old bookmarks/shared links keep working. Nav/footer/homepage's two links (`tailorTrip`→`/quiz`, `tailorMade`→`/tailor-made`) collapsed into one (`planMyTrip`→`/plan-my-trip`); `en.json`/`fr.json` updated (including the homepage's "How it works" step-1 copy, which described the now-gone quiz-matching behavior). Staff booking-detail page's existing `TAILOR_MADE` detail block extended to show `preferredTags`/`preferredSites` when non-empty. Removed `tests/catalog.domain.test.ts`'s `scorePackagesForQuiz` suite and `tests/catalog-public.test.ts`'s `getQuizResults` test; added `CreateTailorMadeInput` preference-field validation tests and two DB-backed API tests (default-empty-array + persisted-when-set) in `tests/api/bookings-v2.api.test.ts`. Schema pushed to the shared Neon DB via `neondb_owner` (used ephemerally, pasted directly by the user for this one command, never written to any file, explicit confirmation beforehand) -- same precedent as every prior schema change this session; verified directly via `psql \d bookings`. Full manual walkthrough via a local dev server (packages → departure → step indicator; `/plan-my-trip` form renders and submits; all three old URLs redirect) plus `lint`/`typecheck`/`build` and the affected test files all green. | V1, V4, V6, V8 |

| DR-047 | 2026-07-18 | Refines `/plan-my-trip` (DR-046) per explicit user feedback on the shipped flow, four changes. **(1) Multi-country selection**: destination country is now checkboxes (was a single `<select>`), one or more required. `Booking.customCountry` (the tax/visa-driving field, unchanged downstream in `invoicingService`/`visaService`) is derived as the *first* selected country; the full ordered selection is kept as new `Booking.preferredCountries String[] @default([])` -- staff context only, same tier as `preferredTags`/`preferredSites`. `CreateTailorMadeInput.customCountry` (single string) replaced by `countries: z.array(z.string().length(2)).min(1)`; `bookingRepository.createTailorMadeRequest` derives `customCountry = countries[0]` with a runtime guard (`noUncheckedIndexedAccess` can't see across the zod `.min(1)` boundary). **(2) Sites/activities now filter by the selected countries**: `DESTINATION_SITES` (already has a `.country` field per site) is filtered client-side to only the guest's selected countries, recomputed reactively as the wizard's country-selection state changes; a site ticked before its country is deselected is dropped. **(3) New required `email` field**, stored as new `Booking.contactEmail String? @db.VarChar(320)` -- deliberately **not** written onto `User.email` (better-auth-managed, `@unique`; two different guests submitting the same real email would collide, and this is contact metadata, not an auth identity change). **(4) The single-page layout became a gradual multi-step wizard**: one question per step (8 steps: destination, dates, travelers, preferences/tags, sites, trip description, special requests, contact), Back/Next buttons, a progress indicator on top -- a new client-managed state machine in `plan-my-trip-form.tsx` (no longer a single native `<form>` submit), with `actions.ts`'s `createPlanMyTripRequestAction` changed to accept a plain typed payload object instead of `FormData` accordingly. Reuses the existing `StepIndicator` component with a new local label set scoped to this form only -- **not** `BOOKING_WIZARD_STEPS` (DR-046's shared array for the direct-package-browse journey), since this flow doesn't participate in that numbered sequence at all. **Fifth, larger change, also from the same user feedback session**: "this is just a request a quotation part... not pay" -- explicit direction to remove the Travelers/Passport/Add-ons/Confirm-&-Pay steps from this stage entirely, ending instead at a generated booking reference. Root-caused as `booking/[bookingId]/page.tsx`'s pre-existing `!setupComplete` gate unconditionally forcing the Continue-setup wizard on ANY fresh booking regardless of origin or quote status -- meaning the page's own already-coded `AWAITING_QUOTATION`/`QUOTATION_SENT` alerts (further down, `setupComplete`-gated) were dead code, unreachable for a zero-traveler `TAILOR_MADE` booking. Fixed with a new top-level branch, scoped to `origin === 'TAILOR_MADE' && status in (AWAITING_QUOTATION, QUOTATION_SENT)`, shown *before* the setup-wizard gate and *before* any `invoicingService.getOrCreateInvoiceForBooking` call (which would 409 -- `getBillableTotal` requires a finished manifest this booking doesn't have): shows the reference code + status-appropriate message (received / quotation ready + accept button) + cancel, nothing else. Deliberately origin-scoped -- a `PREDEFINED_PACKAGE` booking can also reach these two statuses (via "Request a quotation"), but only ever *after* its own setup was already complete and a real invoice already exists, so that path is completely unchanged, still using the original lower-section alerts. Verified end-to-end via raw HTTP against a live dev server (anonymous sign-in → `POST /api/v1/bookings/tailor-made` with `countries: ["NA","ZM"]` → confirmed `customCountry: "NA"` + `preferredCountries: ["NA","ZM"]` + `contactEmail` all persisted correctly → `GET /booking/{id}` confirmed the new confirmation-only screen renders with zero "Continue setup"/"Travelers" wizard content) -- the multi-step wizard's own click-through interaction (Next/Back, per-step validation, country-filtered sites) could not be driven in a real browser in this sandbox (no browser-automation tool available) and was instead verified by full type-checking of all state/handlers plus careful code review; a human should click through it before fully trusting the interactive polish. New/updated tests: `CreateTailorMadeInput` domain tests (multi-country accepted, empty array rejected, missing/malformed email rejected) and new DB-backed API tests in `tests/api/bookings-v2.api.test.ts` (multi-country persistence, contact-email persistence). Staff booking-detail page's `TAILOR_MADE` block extended to show `preferredCountries` (only the entries beyond the first, since the first duplicates the line above) and `contactEmail`. The staff-side "book a tailor-made trip for a client" form (`bookings/new/actions.ts`, unrelated `?tailorMade=1` branch, DR-027) needed a matching but minimal update -- still a single free-text country code, just wrapped as a one-element array to satisfy the widened schema; not rebuilt as a multi-select, and reuses the same email already collected there for `findOrCreateTouristByEmail` rather than adding a second field. Schema (`preferredCountries`, `contactEmail`) pushed to the shared Neon DB via `neondb_owner` (pasted directly by the user for this one command, never written to any file, explicit confirmation beforehand), verified via `psql \d bookings`. `tests/api/bookings-v2.api.test.ts` hit this sandbox's documented intermittent Prisma-to-Neon connectivity gotcha persistently across many retries during this session (confirmed transient via a bare `tsx` script connecting instantly every time) -- one partial run did execute 7 of 11 assertions successfully with zero logic failures (only connectivity errors on the rest), and a manual `psql` check afterward found + is leaving in place two harmless orphaned test-fixture orgs from the failed partial runs (cleanup attempt was correctly blocked by the sandbox's own safety classifier as an unreviewed destructive DB write -- left alone rather than pushed through). Treat this file as needing a real CI run before fully trusting it. `lint`/`typecheck`/`build` all green throughout. | V1, V4, V6, V8 |

| DR-048 | 2026-07-18 | Third `/plan-my-trip` refinement round (after DR-046, DR-047), four explicit user requests. **(1) Trip description is now optional**: `CreateTailorMadeInput.customDescription` dropped its `.min(1)`, now `z.string().max(2000).optional()` -- staff already see country/dates/tags/sites/add-ons context by the time they read it, so a free-text description is a nice-to-have, not required to submit an inquiry. **(2) New "Add-ons" wizard step**: photography/videography/translator/visa-assistance checkboxes (reuses the existing `AddonCode` enum `BookingAddon`/`AddonService` already use, but as new `Booking.preferredAddons AddonCode[] @default([])` -- staff context only, same tier as `preferredTags`/`preferredSites`/`preferredCountries`, since there's no priced `AddonService` catalog entry to attach a real `BookingAddon` row to for a bespoke inquiry with no package yet) plus **country of residence and citizenship** (new `Booking.countryOfResidence`/`citizenship String? @db.VarChar(2)`, ISO-3166 alpha-2, same convention as `Traveler.nationality` but collected from the *guest* at inquiry time rather than per-traveler later -- relevant context for the visa-assistance add-on specifically). `ADDON_CODES` is a new local constant in `booking/domain.ts` (not imported from catalog, unlike `PACKAGE_TAGS` -- catalog doesn't itself export a zod-validating vocabulary for `AddonCode` yet, so there was nothing to reuse). Wizard grew from 8 steps to 9 (`STEPS` array in `plan-my-trip-form.tsx`); the new step's fields are all optional, matching the DR-047 precedent of not blocking progress on non-essential fields. **(3) Confirmed already-correct, no code change**: the staff "send quotation" form only ever asked for amount/currency (never client info), and the customer's Plan My Trip details were already displayed above it since DR-046/047 -- verified by reading the actual form code rather than assuming. **(4) New visibility gate on the main staff Bookings and Itineraries lists**: per explicit user direction, a fresh `TAILOR_MADE` request is "just an inquiry" and shouldn't clutter `/staff/bookings`/`/staff/itineraries` until its quotation is accepted (`AWAITING_DEPOSIT`+) -- before that, it's reachable only via the existing `/staff/quote-requests` pipeline view (which already filtered *to* exactly these two statuses, DR-018-era). New pure `bookingService`-exported predicate `isPendingInquiry(booking)` (`origin === 'TAILOR_MADE' && status in (AWAITING_QUOTATION, QUOTATION_SENT)`) in `booking/domain.ts`, applied as a `.filter()` in both `/staff/bookings/page.tsx` and `/staff/itineraries/page.tsx` (the latter zips itineraries with their booking since `convertToItinerary` only requires a *sent* quotation, not an accepted one, so an `Itinerary` row can technically exist for a still-pending inquiry). Deliberately scoped to `TAILOR_MADE` only and to the `AWAITING_DEPOSIT`+ threshold specifically (both confirmed via explicit user choice over two other options each) -- a `PREDEFINED_PACKAGE` booking is a real booking from creation and is completely unaffected. New tests: `CreateTailorMadeInput` domain tests (description omittable, add-ons/residence/citizenship accepted, invalid add-on code rejected) and a new `isPendingInquiry` domain-test suite (4 cases across both origins and the status boundary); DB-backed API tests in `tests/api/bookings-v2.api.test.ts` for description-omitted and add-ons/residence/citizenship persistence. Verified end-to-end via raw HTTP against a live dev server (anonymous sign-in → create a booking with `preferredAddons`/`countryOfResidence`/`citizenship` and no `customDescription` → confirmed all fields persisted exactly as sent, including `customDescription: null` → confirmed the DR-047 confirmation-only screen still renders correctly) -- one request transiently 404'd from the same documented Prisma-to-Neon connectivity gotcha hitting the dev server's own Prisma client mid-request (confirmed via server logs, not a code defect; succeeded on retry). Staff-side visibility-gate and detail-page display verified by code review + the underlying data verification above, not a live staff session click-through (same no-browser-tool limitation as DR-047). Schema (`preferredAddons`, `countryOfResidence`, `citizenship`) pushed to the shared Neon DB via a user-pasted `neondb_owner` credential (never written to any file), verified via `psql \d bookings`. `tests/api/bookings-v2.api.test.ts` hit the same persistent connectivity gotcha across many retries this session (confirmed transient via a bare `tsx` script each time) and could not be run to a clean pass in-session -- one more harmless orphaned `BOOKINGS-V2-TEST-*` fixture org was left behind by a partial run (cleanup blocked by the sandbox's safety classifier as an unreviewed destructive DB write, same as the two from DR-047, all left in place). Treat this file as needing a real CI run before fully trusting it. `lint`/`typecheck`/`build` all green throughout. | V1, V4, V6, V8 |
| DR-049 | 2026-07-18 | Booking-lifecycle staff workflow simplification: **reverses DR-048's visibility gate**, per explicit user direction after reviewing the accumulated staff-facing surfaces and judging them overengineered. There are exactly 3 booking-creation entry points (guest browsing packages, guest `/plan-my-trip`, staff's own manual "new booking" form -- the last of which produces either origin, same as the two guest flows) and all of them now show up in one place, `/staff/bookings`, distinguished by status alone. **Removed** `isPendingInquiry`/`INQUIRY_ONLY_STATUSES` from `booking/domain.ts` entirely (no longer referenced anywhere) and the filter it powered on both `/staff/bookings` and `/staff/itineraries`. **Removed** `/staff/quote-requests` outright (its own `QUOTE_PIPELINE_STATUSES` filter was already origin-inconsistent with `isPendingInquiry` -- a `PREDEFINED_PACKAGE` booking awaiting a quote appeared on both that page and the main list at once, while a `TAILOR_MADE` one in the same status appeared only there) -- its one bit of value, filtering to a status, is now a status-filter pill row on `/staff/bookings` itself (plus a new Source/origin column so the 3 entry points stay visually distinguishable). Its `StaffNav` link removed; a permanent redirect (`next.config.mjs`) sends any bookmarked `/staff/quote-requests` link to `/staff/bookings`. `/staff/itineraries` gained a Booking-status column instead of hiding a row -- an Itinerary can exist for a still-pending inquiry (`convertToItinerary` only needs a *sent* quotation), and now that's visible rather than hidden. Two adjacent fixes bundled in since staff now see the full status range front-and-center: (1) `bookingRepository.updateStatus`/`sendQuotation` threw a raw `Error` on an invalid transition (e.g. double-submitting Confirm, or Cancel on an already-refunded booking) -- new `InvalidTransitionError` class + a `transition()` helper in `booking/service.ts` catches it and rethrows `Errors.conflict` (409), same pattern `createHold`'s `SoldOutError` already established, applied to all 7 status-changing service methods; (2) staff previously had no way to accept a sent quotation on a client's behalf (e.g. a phone acceptance) -- only the guest could push `QUOTATION_SENT -> AWAITING_DEPOSIT`. New `acceptQuotationAction` (staff `bookings/[bookingId]/actions.ts`) calls the existing `bookingService.acceptQuotation` (no service change needed -- staff already bypass the ownership check inside it) with a new button on the booking-detail page. Deliberately **not** done this pass (explicit user choice, kept focused): deduplicating the 3 hand-copied `CANCELLABLE_STATUSES` arrays, and removing the dead unreachable `DRAFT` enum value. New/updated tests in `tests/api/bookings-v2.api.test.ts` (staff accepting a quotation directly, an invalid transition returning 409 not 500) and removal of the now-dead `isPendingInquiry` describe block in `tests/booking.domain.test.ts`. No schema/RLS change -- purely a service-layer error-handling fix + page-level UI consolidation. `lint`/`typecheck` clean; `tests/booking.domain.test.ts` (52 tests) green. `npm run build` unverifiable this session (sandbox has no network access to fonts.googleapis.com at all, pre-existing and unrelated to this change); `tests/api/bookings-v2.api.test.ts` unverifiable this session (the documented Prisma-to-Neon connectivity gotcha was down persistently, confirmed via a bare `tsx` script, 3 retries) -- both need a real CI run. | V1, V4, V7 |
| DR-050 | 2026-07-19 | Package booking-setup wizard restructured, per explicit user direction (clarified via a scoped question round first: seat count stays on `/book/[departureId]`, unchanged; the two new tour-lead fields live directly on `Traveler`; `sex`/`nationality` stay universal rather than tour-lead-only; the passport rule is exactly "everyone needs one, only if Visa Assistance was picked, nothing more nuanced"). **Reordered**: Add-ons is now the wizard's first step (right after the booking/hold itself exists), then Travelers, then a **new conditional Passport step** -- it only appears at all if the finalized add-ons included Visa Assistance. New `Booking.requiresPassportUpload Boolean @default(false)`, snapshotted inside `bookingService.setAddons` by checking each selected add-on's `AddonService.code` (no new cross-module dependency -- `setAddons` already resolves each one via `catalogService.getAddonService` to price-snapshot it). When required, **every traveler needs a passport uploaded, not just the tour lead** -- a reversal of the DR-015-era rule, including a hardcoded service-layer check in `bookingService.setTravelerPassport` (`if (!traveler.isTourLead) throw ...`) that would otherwise have silently 409'd a real non-lead upload attempt even after the UI allowed it. `domain.isTravelerManifestComplete` gained a third parameter, `requiresPassports: boolean`, replacing its old unconditional "the tour lead needs a passport" check. Two new `Traveler` columns, `email`/`countryOfResidence` (ISO-3166 alpha-2 for the latter, same convention as `nationality`) -- collected only for the tour lead in practice (the wizard only renders those inputs, plus `phone`, when adding the first/only traveler, which is always the lead per the existing `defaultChecked`/`disabled` checkbox mechanic -- no client-side interactivity needed, the branch is resolved server-side from `hasTourLead`). `phone` moved from "optional, asked of every traveler" to "required, tour-lead-only" alongside the two new fields. `disabilities`/`drinkPreference` dropped from the wizard's UI entirely per the user's own enumerated field list (kept as unused-but-not-removed optional `Traveler` columns -- no data-loss risk, just no longer collected). Applied identically to both parallel wizards (guest `(guest)/booking/[bookingId]/...` and staff `staff/.../bookings/[bookingId]/...`, which have always been near-duplicate page/action pairs since DR-015). New `getBookingWizardSteps(requiresPassportUpload)` replaces the static `BOOKING_WIZARD_STEPS` array so the guest-facing step indicator only shows a Passport step when one will actually appear. Updated `tests/api/booking-setup.api.test.ts` (a new Visa-Assistance-coded `AddonService` fixture + a 409-before-selecting/200-after-selecting pair of tests) and both e2e specs (`guest-checkout.spec.ts`, `staff-dashboard.spec.ts`) -- each fixture now seeds a Visa Assistance add-on and the walkthroughs select it, preserving real coverage of the Vercel Blob passport upload (OI-08) rather than letting the reorder silently skip that step. `lint`/`typecheck` clean; `tests/booking.domain.test.ts` (54 tests, including a rewritten `isTravelerManifestComplete` suite covering both the passports-not-required and everyone-not-just-the-lead cases) green. Schema change (`Traveler.email`/`countryOfResidence`, `Booking.requiresPassportUpload`) applied to the shared Neon DB via a user-pasted `neondb_owner` credential (never written to any file) -- Prisma's query engine could not reach either the pooled or direct Neon endpoint from this sandbox (the documented intermittent gotcha, confirmed via both a bare `tsx` `$queryRaw` script and `npx prisma db push` itself, both retried), while a plain `psql` connection to the direct endpoint succeeded immediately, same divergence this gotcha has shown before -- applied the three additive `ALTER TABLE ... ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS` statements by hand via `psql` instead (exact DDL Prisma would have generated for these field additions) and verified via `psql \d travelers`/`\d bookings` that all three columns exist with the expected type/default. No RLS change needed (new columns on already-RLS-protected tables, not a new table). `tests/api/booking-setup.api.test.ts`/e2e still could not be run to completion in this sandbox (Prisma's engine, not just `db push`, can't reach Neon this session) -- both need a real CI run to verify the runtime behavior, though the schema itself is now confirmed live. | V1, V4, V6, V7, V8 |
| DR-051 | 2026-07-19 | Guest package-booking flow polish batch, same session as DR-050, per explicit user direction. **(1) Departure dates are now staff-only**: a package's public detail page (`/packages/[packageId]`) shows a single Available/Unavailable slot for the whole package instead of one row per departure (picks the first bookable departure, or the first one at all if none are bookable, so guests never see multiple visually-identical "Available" rows) -- and `/book/[departureId]`'s own heading, which used to print the raw `startDate`, now shows only the price. Departure dates remain visible in the staff dashboard (`/staff/departures/[departureId]` etc.), unchanged. **(2) Removed "Request a quotation" entirely** -- the button on the guest booking-detail page, `requestQuotationAction`, and `bookingService.requestQuotation` (DR-024) are all gone; a **real pre-existing bug surfaced while removing it**: `domain.ts`'s `TRANSITIONS` table has never actually allowed `AWAITING_DEPOSIT -> AWAITING_QUOTATION` (only `DRAFT` lists that edge), so since DR-049's `transition()` wrapper started converting `InvalidTransitionError` into a clean `Errors.conflict`, every real call to this feature would have silently 409'd -- it was already broken, just never observed (the one DB-backed test exercising it, in `tests/api/bookings.security.test.ts`, couldn't be run all session per the documented Neon connectivity gotcha). That whole test describe block (+ its now-unused `quoteBookingId` fixture and `AuthContext`/`DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS`/`bookingService` imports) is removed too. **(3) "Your details" (`/book/[departureId]`) now asks First name + Last name** instead of one "Your name" field, matching `/plan-my-trip`'s existing convention -- still combined into `User.name` via `authService.updateProfile` (the only place it's stored). **(4) New tour-lead prefill**: the Travelers step's tour-lead-only fields (first/last name, phone) now default from what was already typed on "Your details", via a new `authService.getUser(ctx.userId)` read (no ctx/permission check needed -- a caller can always read their own record) and a new `parseE164`/`toE164`-paired helper in `src/lib/country-codes.ts` that splits a stored E.164 number back into a dial-code/local-number pair (longest-matching-prefix, since several countries share a 1-digit code). **(5) Back navigation**: Plan My Trip's existing (already non-lossy, pure client state) Back button gets a `←` prefix for visual consistency; new `←` links on `/book/[departureId]` (back to the package) and the Travelers step (back to Add-ons); Add-ons itself is now re-editable if revisited after being finalized once, rather than auto-redirecting forward (new read-only `bookingService.listAddons` pre-checks the current selection; resubmitting is always safe since `setAddons` is a replace-all) -- explicit user choice over also supporting edit/remove of an already-added traveler, which would need a separate, not-yet-built capability. Applied identically to the guest and staff wizards throughout. New `tests/lib/country-codes.test.ts` (7 tests, `parseE164`/`toE164` round-trip and edge cases); `e2e/guest-checkout.spec.ts` updated for the First/Last name split and now asserts the tour-lead prefill actually worked (`toHaveValue` before overwriting) instead of just re-typing the same values. `lint`/`typecheck` clean; `tests/booking.domain.test.ts` (54) + `tests/lib/country-codes.test.ts` (7) green. No schema/RLS change. `tests/api/bookings.security.test.ts`/e2e could not be run to completion in this sandbox (same persistent Neon connectivity gotcha) -- needs a real CI run. | V1, V4, V6, V7 |
| DR-052 | 2026-07-19 | **Removed `Booking.confirmationCode` entirely** -- security-relevant business-rule change, per explicit user direction after they noticed the "Your booking" page showing two different-looking-but-same-format codes (`bookingReference` and `confirmationCode`, both 6-char patterns since DR-045) with no obvious reason why. Presented the actual tradeoff before proceeding (bookingReference is already shown non-privately everywhere -- staff pages, invoices, this same screen -- so making it the sole "find my booking" lookup key measurably weakens the anti-enumeration protection DR-016 originally designed in with a genuinely secret second code); user chose to consolidate anyway. `/find-booking` now takes **`bookingReference` + the tour lead's last name** (was `confirmationCode` + last name) -- `bookingService.findByConfirmationCode` removed, reusing the existing `findByBookingReference` (already shared with the ratings module's guest lookup, DR-037). `bookingService.lookupByConfirmationCode` renamed to `lookupByBookingReference`; `LookupBookingInput`'s field renamed to match. `domain.ts`'s `generateConfirmationCode()` -- previously called twice per booking (once per code) -- renamed to `generateBookingReference()` and now called once; `repository.ts`'s `createBookingWithUniqueCodes` renamed to `createBookingWithUniqueReference`, simplified to a single generated value. The crude audit-log-backed rate limit (DR-016, `countRecentAuditEvents`) is now the *only* remaining defense against automated lookup guessing -- documented explicitly in `lookupByBookingReference`'s docstring rather than left implicit. Guest UI: `/find-booking`'s "Reference code" field relabeled "Booking reference"; the "Your booking" page's separate "Your reference code" alert (shown only once a payment was initiated) is gone -- the single `bookingReference` display (already always shown) is now presented larger (`text-3xl font-bold`, matching the DR-047 treatment already used on the TAILOR_MADE inquiry confirmation screen) with a folded-in reminder that the last name + this reference are what's needed for any future lookup/contact. Schema: dropped the `confirmationCode String @unique` column outright (not just stopped using it) -- charter's "delete unused code completely" preference, and keeping a redundant unique column with nothing reading it would just be debt. This touched **29 test fixture files** that raw-created a `Booking` row with both codes (mechanical: drop the `confirmationCode:` line, rename the remaining generator call) plus a full rewrite of `tests/booking-lookup.test.ts` (the core lookup-flow suite, now exercising `bookingReference` + last name) and `e2e/guest-checkout.spec.ts` (one reference captured and reused for both the confirmation display and the later find-booking lookup, instead of scraping two separately-styled codes). `lint`/`typecheck` clean; `tests/booking.domain.test.ts` (54, including the renamed `generateBookingReference` generator suite) green. Schema applied to the shared Neon DB via a user-pasted `neondb_owner` credential (never written to any file) -- ran `ALTER TABLE "bookings" DROP COLUMN "confirmationCode"` directly via `psql` against the direct (non-pooled) endpoint (Prisma's own query engine still couldn't reach Neon from this sandbox this session); confirmed 20 pre-existing booking rows all had a `confirmationCode` value before the drop (now permanently gone, as expected -- nothing reads it anymore), and verified via `psql \d bookings` afterward that the column is gone and `bookingReference` remains `NOT NULL`/`UNIQUE`. `docs/openapi.yaml`'s `Booking` schema updated (also fixed pre-existing staleness: it still said "8-char" and `POL-2026-000154`, both superseded by DR-045). | V1, V4, V6, V7, V8 |
| DR-053 | 2026-07-20 | **Cancelled/refunded bookings hidden from guest + staff views, and a cancelled booking's `bookingReference` is now reusable** -- both requested together in one message. **(1) Visibility**: `bookingService.lookupByBookingReference` now excludes any booking whose status is `CANCELLED`/`REFUNDED` (`CLOSED_BOOKING_STATUSES`) before the last-name check runs -- a closed booking is a dead end for the guest "find my booking" flow, same anti-enumeration posture as an outright wrong reference (no leak of "found it but it's cancelled" vs. "never existed"). `/staff/bookings`'s default ("All") view now filters those two statuses out of the list (`HIDDEN_BY_DEFAULT`) -- still fully reachable via their own status-filter pills (e.g. to find one and mark it refunded), just not cluttering the day-to-day active-bookings view. **(2) Reference reuse**: rather than a partial unique index (rejected -- Prisma's schema DSL can't express a conditional unique constraint declaratively, and dropping `@unique` entirely would force every `findUnique` call site onto an ambiguity-prone `findFirst`), cancelling a booking now **regenerates** its `bookingReference` via new `bookingRepository.cancelAndReleaseReference` (same collision-retry-on-`P2002` shape as `createBookingWithUniqueReference`, DR-045) -- global uniqueness stays intact at all times, and the freed original code re-enters the ~77.7M-combination keyspace for a genuinely new booking to draw instead of staying permanently locked out for a hidden, dead record. `bookingService.cancel` keeps the pre-cancel reference (`previousReference`) for the cancellation notification and audit-log `metadata` -- the guest still sees the code they originally knew in that message, only the DB row's stored value changes. Deliberately scoped to the explicit `cancel()` action only, not the automatic `sweepLifecycle` bulk-SQL hold-expiry sweep (documented in the new repository method) -- giving every auto-expired row its own fresh, collision-checked code would need per-row retry logic inside a single bulk raw-SQL statement, real complexity for a background job with no user waiting on the result. New test coverage: `tests/booking-lookup.test.ts` gained a dedicated fixture (its own booking, separate from the file's shared one) asserting a reference resolves before cancellation and no longer resolves after; `tests/api/bookings-v2.api.test.ts`'s cancel-then-refund test now asserts the returned `bookingReference` changed. No schema/RLS change. `lint`/`typecheck` clean; the two DB-backed test files above could not be run to completion in this sandbox (the documented intermittent Prisma-to-Neon connectivity gotcha, confirmed still transient via a bare `tsx` script connecting fine on the same credentials) -- needs a real CI run to verify. | V1, V4, V6, V7 |
| DR-054 | 2026-07-20 | **Guest-chosen travel dates now determine (create) the Departure for a `PREDEFINED_PACKAGE` booking**, per explicit user choice among two options presented (informational-only dates vs. this bigger change) -- booking a package stops being tied to a pre-scheduled `Departure` picked from a fixed list; a fresh one is created scoped to exactly the guest's own dates and seat count, much closer to how a `TAILOR_MADE` request already worked. New `catalogService.createDepartureForBooking(ctx, packageId, { startDate, endDate, capacity })` -- gated on `catalog.read` (not `catalog.write`, since a `TOURIST` triggers this themselves as part of booking, not administering the catalog), requires the package `PUBLISHED` + priced, rejects `endDate <= startDate`, and creates a real `Departure` with a real `tourPackageId` (capacity == the booking's own seat count, same "exists for one group, not public sale" precedent as `createBespokeDeparture`'s package-less bespoke departure, DR-028 -- the difference here is a real package join supplies price/currency/country, nothing is snapshotted onto the row). New `bookingService.createHoldWithDates` + `CreateBookingWithDatesInput` zod schema (`packageId`/`startDate`/`endDate`/`seats`/optional `touristUserId`/`specialRequests`, `.refine`d so `endDate > startDate`) -- extracted a shared `finalizeHold` helper (price + capacity-check + persist + audit) out of the existing `createHold` so both the departureId-based and dates-based paths share the exact same pricing/capacity/audit logic, avoiding duplication. New guest route `/book-package/[packageId]` (page + client `booking-form.tsx` + `createGuestPackageBookingAction`), modeled closely on the existing `/book/[departureId]` trio -- collects travel start/end dates (HTML date inputs, `min` = today) alongside seats/name/phone, same anonymous-session-then-Server-Action shape. `/packages/[packageId]` now links to this new route instead of a specific departure, and its own "Available/Unavailable" badge is now a package-level question (published + priced) rather than "is there an open slot on some pre-existing departure right now" -- capacity/dates are no longer shown there at all (nothing shared to report). **Deliberately additive, not a replacement**: `/book/[departureId]`, `CreateBookingInput`, `bookingService.createHold`, and `POST /api/v1/bookings` are all completely unchanged and still used by the staff `/staff/bookings/new` flow (which still picks a real, staff-managed departure -- sensible for group/staff-context bookings) and by `/api/v1/bookings` API consumers; nothing about this DR touches them. New `tests/booking.domain.test.ts` coverage for `CreateBookingWithDatesInput` (valid range accepted, `endDate <= startDate` rejected, non-positive seats rejected, malformed `packageId` rejected, optional fields accepted -- 59 tests total, green) and a new DB-backed `tests/booking-guest-dates.test.ts` (fresh-Departure-per-booking, capacity == seats, price inherited not snapshotted, two guests booking the same package get two different Departures, DRAFT package rejected, bad date range rejected) -- 3 of 4 cases ran clean against the real Neon DB in this session (confirming the actual behavior, not just the domain-level validation), the 4th only needed a longer per-test timeout (bumped to 40s, two sequential multi-round-trip calls) and could not be re-verified afterward due to the documented intermittent Prisma-to-Neon connectivity gotcha recurring -- needs a real CI run to fully confirm. No schema/RLS change (`Departure`/`priceOverrideMinor`/`customCountry` etc. all already existed, DR-028/029). `lint`/`typecheck` clean throughout. **Revised same session, per explicit user correction**: a package's trip length is staff-set (`TourPackage.durationDays`, an already-existing optional field, set on the package create/edit form), not something a guest varies per booking. The guest-facing input dropped `endDate` entirely -- `CreateBookingWithDatesInput` is now `{ packageId, startDate, seats, touristUserId?, specialRequests? }`, and `catalogService.createDepartureForBooking`'s params dropped `endDate` too, now computing it via new pure `computeDepartureEndDate(startDate, durationDays)` (1 day = same start/end date; N days = N calendar days inclusive, start through start+N-1). `createDepartureForBooking` now also requires `pkg.durationDays != null` (alongside `PUBLISHED` + priced) before treating a package as bookable, and `catalogService.updatePackage` blocks publishing a package with no duration set, mirroring the existing no-price block (DR-039) -- both nullable-until-set, both grandfathered for packages that predate this rule. `/book-package/[packageId]`'s form now collects only a start date (plus a display-only "you'll return on..." client-side preview computed with the same formula, never authoritative). Updated/added tests in `tests/booking.domain.test.ts`, `tests/catalog.domain.test.ts` (new `computeDepartureEndDate` coverage), and `tests/booking-guest-dates.test.ts` (added a case rejecting a published, priced package with no duration set). No schema/RLS change (`durationDays` already existed on `TourPackage`); `lint`/`typecheck` clean. | V1, V4, V6, V7 |
| DR-055 | 2026-07-20 | **Real Resend confirmation email on `/plan-my-trip` (TAILOR_MADE) request creation** -- the first live-tested notification send in this codebase; every notification event since DR-013 shipped env-gated against not-yet-provisioned credentials (OI-05/06/07). A real, working `RESEND_API_KEY` was already sitting unused in `.env`/`.env.local`, confirmed live with a direct curl call to Resend's own `delivered@resend.dev` test address (returned a real message id) before any code was written. New `NotificationEvent` variant `TAILOR_MADE_REQUEST_RECEIVED` with EN/FR templates summarizing the request (destination countries, traveler count, travel dates) and calling out the `bookingReference` the guest will need for `/find-booking` (DR-052) and to accept a quotation. New `notificationsService.notifyEmail(event, email, locale, organizationId, data)` -- a second public method alongside the existing `notify()`, sending straight to an explicit email address via only the `EMAIL` gateway (no WhatsApp/SMS fallback is possible without a phone number) rather than resolving a recipient from a `User` row. This is necessary, not a style choice: the guest's real, typed-in address is `Booking.contactEmail`, deliberately never written onto `User.email` (DR-047's own reasoning -- an anonymous guest session's `User.email` is a synthetic, unusable placeholder, and two guests sharing a real email would otherwise collide on `User.email`'s `@unique` constraint). `bookingService.createTailorMadeRequest` gained an optional `locale: Locale = 'EN'` parameter (the module has no cookie/session access of its own) and now fires the email immediately after creating the booking, fire-and-forget per charter rule 8 (a channel outage must never fail the booking) -- covers all three entry points that call this method (guest `/plan-my-trip`, the `POST /api/v1/bookings/tailor-made` route, and staff's own manual tailor-made booking creation), not just the guest flow. The `/plan-my-trip` Server Action reads the guest's own `locale` cookie (DR-023) and maps it to the notifications module's uppercase `Locale` enum. New `tests/api/bookings-v2.api.test.ts` case (asserts the mocked `EMAIL` gateway receives the guest's typed address, the real `bookingReference`, and the selected countries) and new `tests/notifications.domain.test.ts` render coverage (EN/FR distinctness, field interpolation, placeholder fallback when countries/dates are absent). No schema/RLS change. `lint`/`typecheck` clean; pure-domain and gateway-mocked API tests green; the DB-backed half of `tests/api/bookings-v2.api.test.ts` hit the documented intermittent Prisma-to-Neon connectivity gotcha this session (confirmed transient -- `psql` connected fine on the same credentials) and needs a real CI run. **Follow-up the same day**: the user tried requesting a quote live on the actual site and received no email. Root-caused via a direct curl call against the real Resend API (not a code bug): the Resend account has no verified sending domain, so Resend sandboxes it to delivering only to the account owner's own address (`cyberpolco@gmail.com`) -- any other recipient gets a 403 (`"You can only send testing emails to your own email address... To send emails to other recipients, please verify a domain at resend.com/domains..."`). `notifyEmail` behaved exactly as designed (attempted the send, caught the rejection, never failed the booking) -- this is an account-configuration gap, not an app defect, and is genuinely blocked on a human (domain verification requires adding SPF/DKIM DNS records at the registrar). See OI-05's updated entry. | V1, V5, V9 |
| DR-056 | 2026-07-20 | **Africa's Talking SMS confirmation on `/plan-my-trip` request creation**, alongside DR-055's email. Confirmed the account is real and live via a no-cost balance-check API call (`GET /version1/user` -- no message sent) before writing code: `USD 0.0621` balance -- low enough that the human should top it up before relying on this in practice (flagged, not assumed). New `notificationsService.notifySms(event, phone, locale, organizationId, data)` -- same "explicit recipient, not a `User` lookup" shape as `notifyEmail`, sent via only the `SMS` gateway. Unlike `notifyEmail`, this reads `User.phone` directly (no new `Booking.contactPhone` column needed) -- the wizard's contact step already writes a real phone there via `authService.updateProfile` before booking creation, so (unlike `User.email`, synthetic for an anonymous session) it's a reliable value. New, separate `SMS_TEMPLATES`/`renderSmsMessage` (plain text, no HTML) -- the email template's `<br>`/`<strong>` markup can't be reused verbatim for a channel with no HTML rendering at all; only events actually sent by SMS need an entry (`renderSmsMessage` returns `null` for the rest). Best-effort and silent when the tourist has no phone on file. New API test (mocked gateway, asserts 2 sends -- email + SMS -- when a phone is present, vs. 1 in DR-055's own test where the fixture tourist has none) and domain render coverage. No schema/RLS change. `lint`/`typecheck` clean; pure-domain tests green. | V1, V5, V9 |
| DR-057 | 2026-07-20 | **Fixed `/find-booking` for a fresh `/plan-my-trip` reference -- was completely, unconditionally broken**, found from the user's own live report ("Find my booking isn't functional with Plan my trip booking reference"), not a hypothetical. Root cause: `bookingService.lookupByBookingReference`'s last-name check only ever compared against the tour-lead `Traveler` row -- but a TAILOR_MADE booking still `AWAITING_QUOTATION`/`QUOTATION_SENT` has **no** Traveler manifest at all yet (the setup wizard only starts once a quotation is accepted), so `lead` was always `undefined` and the lookup rejected *every* such reference regardless of how correct the last name was. This had been broken since `/plan-my-trip` first shipped (DR-046/047) and went uncaught because `tests/booking-lookup.test.ts`'s only fixture had a real Traveler from the start -- no test exercised the pre-Traveler-manifest case.
