| DR-197 | 2026-08-30 | **User-reported bug**: the staff Tracking page's "Active Trips" list (`trackingService.getFleetSnapshot`) is built purely from `Assignment` rows joined to their `Departure`'s own `startDate`/`endDate`, with no reference to `Booking` at all -- neither `Assignment` nor `Departure` carries a `bookingId`/cascade back to the booking(s) that actually drove them, and `bookingService.deleteBooking` only ever soft-deletes the `Booking` row itself (confirmed: no existing cleanup path touches `Departure`/`Assignment` on booking deletion, unlike the itinerary/visa/fleet-availability cleanup DR-059/DR-151/DR-149 already added for the same event). Net effect: deleting a departure's only booking left a "ghost" active trip on screen -- vehicle/driver/guide still shown against a date-range that still reads `IN_PROGRESS` -- until the departure's own `endDate` finally passed. Fixed by having `getFleetSnapshot` skip any `departureId` that fails `bookingService.hasActiveBookingForDeparture` (the same live-booking check `syncFleetAvailabilityForDeparture`/DR-082 already uses to resync fleet resources on this exact event) before computing trip progress for it. **New module dependency: `tracking` now imports `booking`** (`{auth, assignment, booking, catalog, fleet}`, was `{auth, assignment, catalog, fleet}`) -- confirmed acyclic: `booking` itself only imports `{auth, catalog, notifications}`, never `tracking`. The tracking page itself has no cache to invalidate (recomputed fresh on every load, see its own file-header comment), so the fix takes effect on the very next page read after a delete, cancel, or refund -- not just delete. `tests/api/tracking.api.test.ts`/`tracking.security.test.ts` fixtures now attach a live `CONFIRMED` booking to every departure they expect to appear as an active trip (previously bare Assignment+Departure rows with no booking at all, which this fix would otherwise now exclude); a new regression test confirms a trip drops out of `activeTrips` immediately once its sole booking is soft-deleted. No schema/permission change. | V1, V3 |
| DR-198 | 2026-08-30 | **Explicit user request**: a guest whose travel date is under a configured lead-time threshold (seeded default: 21 days) may no longer pay a deposit -- only full payment, at a surcharge (seeded default: 5%) on top. Applies to both booking origins: `PREDEFINED_PACKAGE` (against the guest-picked `Departure.startDate`, decided in `bookingService.finalizeHold` -- shared by `createHold`/`createHoldWithDates`, so it also covers the legacy staff-facing pre-scheduled-departure path) and `TAILOR_MADE` (against `customTravelStart`, decided in `createTailorMadeRequest` at wizard-submission time, explicit user choice -- **not** re-evaluated later when staff sends the quote, even though invoice creation for that origin can happen much later). New platform-wide, effective-dated `LateBookingRate` model (`thresholdDays`/`surchargeRateBp`, no `organizationId`/RLS -- same precedent as `TaxRate`/`PlatformRate`), CRUD'd by the `settings` module (new `/staff/settings/late-booking-rate` page, gated by the existing `platform_settings.read`/`.write` permissions, no new permission needed) -- deliberately **not** wired into `financeService.reapplyRatesToAllCostBreakdowns` like a `TaxRate`/`PlatformRate` change is (DR-136/146): the whole point of this rate is a frozen-at-creation-time snapshot, so a later rate change must never retroactively touch an existing booking/invoice. The decision is snapshotted as `Booking.lateBookingSurchargeBp` (nullable, same "snapshot onto the row, not a live FK" convention as `priceTaxRateBp`), then read by `invoicingService.getOrCreateInvoiceForBooking` and folded into `computeInvoiceAmounts` (`invoicing/domain.ts`) as its own itemized line -- on top of subtotal+tax+platform fee (explicit user choice: not itself taxed), after which `splitDeposit` is skipped entirely (`depositMinor = totalMinor`, `balanceMinor = 0`). `Invoice` also stores the rate itself (`lateBookingSurchargeRateBp`), not just the computed `lateBookingSurchargeMinor` -- same "rate + amount, both persisted" convention as `taxRateBp`/`platformFeeRateBp` -- needed so `applyCoupon`/`removeCoupon`'s recompute (which only has the invoice row in hand, not the booking) doesn't silently wipe the surcharge back out; a real bug caught and fixed during implementation, before it ever shipped. Server-side enforcement is in `invoicing/domain.ts`'s `canInitiatePayment`, which now rejects a `DEPOSIT` payment outright once `Invoice.depositAllowed` is false, regardless of what the guest checkout UI shows (charter rule 1) -- confirmed against a real payment-route call in `tests/api/late-booking-payment.security.test.ts`. **Module-dependency note**: the new rate table's read side lives in a shared `src/lib/late-booking-rate.ts` helper (same tier as `platform-rate.ts`/`addon-rates.ts`), not a `settings` module import from `booking` -- `settings` already transitively depends on `booking` (via `finance`), so `booking` importing `settings` directly would have been circular; this avoids that entirely, with zero new module-to-module edges. Both guest date-picker steps (`book-package/[packageId]`, `plan-my-trip`) show a live, preview-only warning (fetching the effective rate server-side, degrading gracefully to no warning if unconfigured -- charter rule 8) before submission; real enforcement is always server-side regardless. EN/FR i18n added throughout; seed.ts seeds the initial 21-day/5% row the same way it seeds `PlatformRate`. No new RLS policy (comment added to `rls.sql` next to `tax_rates`/`platform_rates`, same reasoning). | V1, V2, V3, V6 |
| DR-199 | 2026-08-30 | **Explicit user confirmation, resolves OI-02 and OI-03.** (1) The guest-facing/staff-portal name split DR-168 already shipped in code -- the guest site displays "Mufasa Safaris & Tours"; the staff portal and every internal module/identifier keep "POLCO Tours" -- is now confirmed to be the **permanent** brand structure, not a placeholder pending trademark clearance. This resolves OI-02's specific concern (an existing Greek tourism brand + a US "Polco" colliding with a *public* launch under "polcotours"/"POLCO TOURS"): since the public-facing brand was never actually that name once DR-168 shipped, the collision risk doesn't apply to what launches publicly. This is **not** a claim that "Mufasa Safaris & Tours" itself has undergone its own trademark clearance -- nobody has raised that as a separate concern, so no new open item was created for it; `polcotours.com` itself still does not resolve as a domain (an unrelated DNS/registration fact, no longer gated on trademark status). (2) Lam has obtained all necessary per-market legal registration documents (Namibia NTB/BIPA/NamRA; DRC DARA/DGI/Ministry of Tourism), resolving OI-03 -- no longer blocks go-live. No schema/permission/module-dependency change -- a brand/legal-status confirmation, not a code change (the same-day `/contact` redesign + real Namibia/DRC office address/email/phone entry that prompted this conversation shipped separately, untagged, since it was pure content/presentation). | V1, V11 |
| DR-200 | 2026-08-30 | **Explicit user request**: the guest footer's social media icons (Facebook/Instagram/X/WhatsApp/TikTok, TikTok itself added same-day) are now staff-editable from `/staff/cms`'s new Social links tab, replacing the hardcoded `SOCIAL_LINKS` array `footer.tsx` previously held. Reuses `CmsMediaItem` (page='social-links') rather than a new table -- the same generic per-page CRUD that already backs `home-hero`/`gallery`/`partners` -- adding one additive, nullable column, `platform` (a plain zod-validated string against a fixed enum `CMS_SOCIAL_PLATFORMS`, same "no Postgres enum" convention as `mediaType`/`country`; every existing row of every other page keeps `platform=null`, unaffected). No image upload for this page -- the icon itself stays a fixed hand-drawn SVG in `footer.tsx`, now keyed by `platform` instead of array position; adding a 6th platform is still a code change (new enum entry + icon path), not staff-configurable free text, so the icon set stays a closed, hand-drawn set (no icon-library dependency added). `url` doubles as the profile link (already nullable on `CmsMediaItem`) -- unset until staff adds the real handle, same placeholder convention the old hardcoded array used (`href: '#'`). The guest footer's public read (`cmsService.listPublicMediaItems('social-links')`) degrades to the same 5-icon/`href:'#'` placeholder set on any read failure or until staff configures at least one real link (charter rule 8's graceful-degradation spirit, applied to our own DB read). No RLS change (`cms_media_items` is already platform-wide/no-RLS) and no module-boundary change (`cms`'s repository/service CRUD was already page-generic; only `domain.ts`'s zod schemas and the staff actions/UI are new). Schema change applied by hand against the shared Neon DB per the usual convention (`ALTER TABLE cms_media_items ADD COLUMN platform TEXT`, verified via `psql` -- `prisma db push`'s own network client repeatedly failed to reach Neon from this session's sandbox, a recurrence of the documented intermittent-Prisma-from-sandbox gotcha; `psql` on the identical credentials connected immediately both before and after). | V1, V6, V9, V11 |
| DR-201 | 2026-08-30 | **Real reported bug (user-reported: "Browse packages on mobile obstruct the footer part Admin access"), root-caused and fixed.** `StickyMobileCta` (`src/components/StickyMobileCta.tsx`, DR-068) -- the homepage-only, mobile-only (`sm:hidden`) "Browse packages" bar -- is `position: fixed` to the *viewport* bottom, so once a mobile guest scrolls to the true end of the page it sits on top of the guest footer's last row (Terms / Admin access links, `footer.tsx`). The homepage's own `pb-24` (`(guest)/page.tsx`) only reserves room *inside* `&lt;main&gt;` for its own last section -- it does nothing for `GuestFooter`, which renders as a sibling *after* `&lt;main&gt;` in the shared `(guest)/layout.tsx`, so the footer's true bottom edge had no reserved clearance at all. Fixed with a plain-CSS `:has()` rule in `globals.css` -- `main:has(.sticky-mobile-cta) + footer { padding-bottom: calc(3.75rem + max(0.75rem, env(safe-area-inset-bottom))) }`, gated to the same sub-640px width where the bar itself is visible -- rather than a flat padding value on `GuestFooter` itself, so any *other* guest page's footer (none of which currently render this bar) gets no unneeded extra padding, and any *future* page that reuses `StickyMobileCta` is automatically covered with no further change needed. `StickyMobileCta`'s root `&lt;div&gt;` gained a plain marker class (`sticky-mobile-cta`, no styling of its own) purely so this selector has something to target. The `calc()` mirrors the bar's own inline height formula (`pt-3` + its `Button`'s `py-3`/`text-sm` content height + its 1px border, plus the identical `max(0.75rem, env(safe-area-inset-bottom))` term the bar itself uses) rather than a guessed flat value, so it stays correct on a notched phone's taller safe-area inset too. No data model/module/permission change -- a CSS layout fix. | V4, V11 |
| DR-202 | 2026-08-30 | **Homepage "Where we operate" map made staff-editable via `/staff/cms`, replacing the DR-034 hardcoded Namibia/DRC/Zambia/Zimbabwe-only highlight set.** New platform-wide, no-`organizationId`/RLS table `CmsOperatingCountry` (same precedent as `CmsTextBlock`/`CmsFaqEntry`) -- `countryCode` (ISO 3166-1 alpha-2), plus hover-tooltip snapshot facts (`capital`/`languages`/`currency`/`population`/`areaKm2`, all plain strings) and a staff-set `sortOrder`. `countryCode` is validated against a new full 55-AU-member-state `AFRICA_COUNTRIES` list (`src/lib/africa-country-ids.ts`, alpha-2/numeric-3/name for every country, replacing the old four-country-only id set) -- deliberately the *full* continent list, not the narrower 4-country `OPERATING_COUNTRY_CODES` business-eligibility set used elsewhere (booking/visa/tax): this table is a decorative map highlight, adding a row here does not open a country for real bookings. `AfricaMap` (`src/components/AfricaMap.tsx`) now takes `operatingCountries` as a prop instead of importing a hardcoded set, and the old `src/lib/country-facts.ts` (the hardcoded four-country fact table) is deleted outright -- fully superseded, no remaining callers. The homepage (`(guest)/page.tsx`) reads `cmsService.listPublicOperatingCountries()` (new public no-ctx method, same convention as `listPublicMediaItems`) and degrades to a 4-country fallback (the original Namibia/DRC/Zambia/Zimbabwe facts, now a plain constant instead of the deleted `country-facts.ts`) on any DB hiccup or until staff configures at least one real row (charter rule 8) -- never a blank/broken map section. New `/staff/cms` tab ("Where we operate", `home-map`) lets staff add any not-yet-added African country from a dropdown (identity fixed at create, same "add blank then edit" limitation as partners/social-links) and edit its facts/order in place, reusing the existing `cms.read`/`cms.write` permissions -- no new permission. Every create/update/delete is audited (`cms.operating_country_created`/`_updated`/`_deleted`). No module-boundary change (stays inside `cms`); no new RLS policy needed (comment on the model documents why, same as `CmsTextBlock`/`CmsFaqEntry`). **Schema change (new table) drafted in `prisma/schema.prisma` but not yet applied to the shared Neon DB as of this entry** -- needs an explicit-confirmed `prisma db push` (+ `prisma generate`, already run locally to unblock typecheck) before this code can deploy without erroring on every homepage/`/staff/cms` load; flagged to the user rather than pushed unilaterally, per the "schema changes to the shared DB need explicit confirmation first" convention (see Gotchas in `CLAUDE.md`). | V1, V6, V9, V11 |
| DR-203 | 2026-08-30 | **User request: the `/staff/cms` Gallery tab gains eyebrow/title/body text editing.** DR-164 already had the guest `/gallery` page read a `CmsTextBlock` keyed `gallery` (`cms?.eyebrow/title/body`, falling back to the static `Gallery` i18n strings), but never gave staff a way to write it -- that tab only ever managed the `CmsMediaItem` site grid. Fixed by adding a `PageTextEditor`/`updatePageTextAction` instance for `cmsKey="gallery"` above the existing site grid, the exact same reusable component every other "thin" page (Packages, Plan my trip, Find booking, Contact, Rate, Weather, Terms) already uses -- no new component, table, permission, or module-boundary change. `GUEST_PATH_BY_TEXT_KEY` (`cms/actions.ts`) gains a `gallery: '/gallery'` entry so saving now revalidates the guest page too (previously absent, so a direct DB write to this key -- there was no UI path to make one -- would not have invalidated the cache). The tab's existing static heading was renamed `gallerySitesSectionTitle` ("Destination sites"/"Sites de destination") to distinguish it from the new editor's own `gallerySectionTitle` ("Gallery") heading, both EN/FR. No schema/permission/module-dependency change. | V1, V6 |
| DR-204 | 2026-08-30 | **User request: the guest footer's closing "© {year} Mufasa Safaris & Tours, a Cyber PolCo Product." line gets a staff-editable section under `/staff/cms`, new "Footer legal line" tab.** Scope, clarified mid-request by the user ("Cyber PolCo is a link must also be edited as an URL"): the year and brand name stay hardcoded in `footer.tsx` (same "brand name is fixed everywhere" convention DR-168 already set) -- only the "Cyber PolCo" link's text and href are staff-editable. Reuses the existing `CmsTextBlock` (new key `footer.legal`) rather than a new table/column: `title` holds the link label, `body` holds its href, same "repurpose the existing eyebrow/title/body shape" convention as Contact's office blocks (DR-164). `body` gets its own dedicated Server Action (`updateFooterLegalAction`, not the generic `updatePageTextAction`) so the href is `zod`-`.url()`-validated server-side, not just via the form's client-side `type="url"` (charter rule 1 -- frontend validates for UX only). `PageTextEditor` (`staff/cms/page-text-editor.tsx`) gains two new optional props to support this without a bespoke form: `bodyType='url'` (renders body as a single-line `<input type="url">` instead of the default textarea) and `formAction` (overrides the default action per-instance) -- both default to prior behavior, so every existing "thin" page editor is unaffected. The guest footer (`footer.tsx`) reads `cmsService.getPublicTextBlock('footer.legal', locale)` and degrades to the original hardcoded label/href (`"Cyber PolCo"` / `https://www.cyberpolco.com`) on any read failure or until staff configures a real row (charter rule 8), same convention as the social-links/partners degradation. Saving revalidates `'/', 'layout'` (not a single guest path) since the footer renders on every guest page via the shared `(guest)` layout, same reasoning as `social-links`' own `revalidateMediaPage` special case. No schema/permission/module-dependency change -- reuses `cms.read`/`cms.write` and the platform-wide, no-RLS `CmsTextBlock` table as-is. | V1, V6 |
| DR-205 | 2026-08-30 | **User request: full notification-event audit + Resend wiring.** Resolves **OI-05** -- `mufasasafaris.com` is now a verified Resend sending domain (`RESEND_FROM_EMAIL="Mufasa Safaris & Tours <info@mufasasafaris.com>"`, set locally and required in Vercel Production/Preview); real recipients no longer 403 (previously sandboxed to the account owner's own address only). `NotificationEvent` grows from 11 to 26: guest-side gaps that previously had no notification at all now do -- `QUOTATION_ACCEPTED` (booking/service.ts `acceptQuotation`), `BOOKING_REFUNDED` (`refund`), `INVOICE_ISSUED` (invoicing `getOrCreateInvoiceForBooking`), `VISA_SUBMITTED` (visa `submitApplication`+`autoSubmitOnPassportUpload`), `VISA_RESUBMITTED` (`resubmitApplication`+`resubmitApplicationForGuest`), `RATING_THANK_YOU` (ratings `submitRating`), `ITINERARY_APPROVED` (itinerary `approveItinerary`, new `itinerary -> notifications` dependency, confirmed acyclic). `RATING_CODE_ISSUED` (`issueRatingCode`) gains a second, explicit `notifyEmail` copy straight to `Booking.contactEmail` alongside its existing `notify()` call -- same class of gap DR-055 already fixed for `TAILOR_MADE_REQUEST_RECEIVED` (an anonymous-checkout `User.email` can be a synthetic placeholder); also fixes a pre-existing inconsistency where this one event passed the raw booking uuid instead of `bookingReference` like every sibling event. New staff-facing events, explicit user request ("notifications aren't only sent to guests but staff too"): `ASSIGNMENT_NOTICE_DRIVER`/`_GUIDE`/`_VEHICLE_OWNER` (assignment `createAssignment`, new `assignment -> notifications` dependency, confirmed acyclic -- notifications never imports assignment), `STAFF_ACCOUNT_DEACTIVATED`/`_REACTIVATED` and `STAFF_PASSWORD_ISSUED`/`_RESET` (all four called from the admin/users Server Actions, deliberately NOT from inside `authService` itself -- `notifications/service.ts` already imports `@modules/auth`, so `auth -> notifications` would be a real cycle; same "one level up" cross-module orchestration convention CLAUDE.md documents), and `VISA_QUEUE_NEW_APPLICATION` (visa `submitApplication`+`autoSubmitOnPassportUpload`, alerting every `VISA_FACILITATOR` in the org via a new `authService.listUsersByRole` -- visa's first *runtime* `@modules/auth` dependency, was type-only before; confirmed acyclic). **Explicit user decision, reversing an existing security control:** `STAFF_PASSWORD_ISSUED`/`_RESET` email the real temporary password in plaintext -- `authService.createUser`/`resetPassword`'s own comments previously documented "never persisted in plaintext or retrievable again, relay out of band"; the user explicitly asked for it emailed anyway. Two mitigations kept: `mustChangePassword` still always forces a change on first sign-in (bounding the exposure window), and both events go out via `notifyEmail` only (no WhatsApp/SMS fallback copy). **Real bug found and fixed:** `notify()` (`notifications/service.ts`) was sending the *same* rendered body to whichever channel won the WhatsApp→SMS→Email race -- harmless while every body was one plain sentence, but every event's HTML body is now a full branded document (see below), so WHATSAPP/SMS would otherwise show raw markup as literal message text. Fixed: EMAIL sends `renderMessage`'s HTML; WHATSAPP/SMS use `renderSmsMessage`'s existing plain-text map, skipping to the next fallback channel (not erroring) when an event has none -- `SMS_TEMPLATES` coverage expanded accordingly to every event realistically reachable by phone. **Explicit user request: every notification email (not just new ones) now renders through a shared branded HTML shell**, new `src/modules/notifications/email-template.ts` (`renderBrandedEmail`) -- Horizon design tokens (navy/amber/bone/mist/rule), the real brand logo (`src/lib/brand-logo.ts`'s existing base64 data URI, no new asset), a generic bold-sans stack rather than the site's own self-hosted webfonts (most email clients strip `@font-face`/block the fetch), and -- per explicit user request -- an identical sign-off and "this is an automated message, please do not reply" notice on every single email, built into the shell itself rather than per-template opt-in so it can never be forgotten on a future event. Wordmark/footer contact differs by an `audience: 'guest' \| 'staff'` flag keyed off each event (`GUEST_EVENTS` set in domain.ts): guest mail says "Mufasa Safaris & Tours" (superseding DR-168's original "transactional notification copy... intentionally keep POLCO TOURS" exclusion -- moot after DR-199 confirmed the brand split is permanent, and the user asked for it explicitly branded this time); staff mail keeps "POLCO Tours." `RATING_CODE_ISSUED`'s guest-facing link also moves off the still-unresolved `polcotours.com` domain onto the real one, `mufasasafaris.com`. | V1, V4, V6, V7, V8 |
| DR-206 | 2026-08-31 | **User request, partially reverses DR-118's "no change to `/book-package/[packageId]`" scope limit.** The guest "Book this trip" link (`(guest)/packages/[packageId]/page.tsx`) now builds its href as `/book-package/${pkg.slug ?? pkg.id}`, the same slug-preferred convention `package-card.tsx` already uses for `/packages/[packageId]` -- no route/schema change, since `catalogService.getPublicPackageWithDepartures` (used by both routes) already accepts either the slug or the raw id (DR-118). One real constraint surfaced by this: `CreateBookingWithDatesInput.packageId` (`booking/domain.ts`) is a strict `z.string().uuid()`, so a slug can never reach `createGuestPackageBookingAction`/`bookingService.createHoldWithDates` -- `(guest)/book-package/[packageId]/page.tsx` now passes the resolved `pkg.id` (not the raw route param) into `BookingForm`, so the URL can show the pretty slug while the actual booking submission still uses the real uuid underneath. No schema/permission/module-dependency change. | V1, V6 |
| DR-207 | 2026-08-31 | **Explicit user request (multi-part).** (1) `/terms` gets real content for the first time — OI-02/OI-03 (both resolved by DR-199) were the only reason the page stayed an honest placeholder, so that reason no longer applies. Restructured from one flat `CmsTextBlock` (`'terms'`) into 4 tabbed sections, `?tab=tos\|privacy\|cookies\|cancellation` (plain `<Link>`s, no client JS, same real-navigation convention as `/staff/cms`, DR-165), each its own `CmsTextBlock` key (`terms.tos`/`terms.privacy`/`terms.cookies`/`terms.cancellation`) with a coded EN/FR default in `messages/en.json`+`fr.json` and an optional staff override from `/staff/cms`'s Terms tab (now 4 stacked `PageTextEditor`s instead of 1) — no schema change, `CmsTextBlock`'s `key` was already a free-form string. The Terms-of-Service section's liability/governing-law paragraphs are explicitly marked `[NEEDS LEGAL REVIEW]` in the copy itself rather than fabricated as final. (2) A real Cancellation & Refund Policy, decided in-conversation against what the code already does (the existing 40% deposit split, `invoicing/domain.ts`'s `splitDeposit`): guest-cancels tiers by days-before-departure — 60+ days: full refund minus the deposit; 30-59: 50%; 14-29: 25%; under 14 (or no date pinned yet): none — plus a 100% operator-cancellation (force-majeure) tier for staff-initiated cancellations, and visa-assistance/government-visa fees are never refundable regardless of tier. `booking/domain.ts` gains `resolveCancellationRefundTier` (pure day-diff, mirrors `computeLateBookingSurchargeBp`'s convention) and a new `CancellationRefundTier` enum; `invoicing/domain.ts` gains `computeCancellationRefundAmountMinor` (tier + what was actually paid -> a real minor-unit amount, capped at what was paid). (3) A real guest self-service cancel/refund flow, added to the bottom of the Find My Booking result page (`(guest)/find-booking/result`) — deliberately **heavily gated**, not a one-click action: the live tier is shown unconditionally, "Cancel this booking" reveals a form requiring the guest's email + a written reason, and the submit button stays disabled until an "I understand" checkbox is ticked. The write itself, `bookingService.cancelForBookingLookup` (new, no-ctx, its own `booking.cancel_via_lookup` rate-limit bucket via `assertWriteNotRateLimited`), re-verifies bookingReference **+ lastName + the tour lead's on-file email** server-side (a real second-factor tightening over `lookupByBookingReference`'s reference+lastName-only trust boundary, since this is a write, not a read) — never trusts what rendered the page a moment earlier. `Booking` gains 3 nullable columns (`cancellationReason`, `cancellationContactEmail`, `cancellationRefundTier` — the tier snapshotted at cancel time, same "snapshot the rate onto the row" precedent as DR-198's `lateBookingSurchargeBp`) and `Invoice` gains one (`refundAmountMinor` — the computed amount, same "rate on Booking, money on Invoice" split DR-198 established). (4) `invoicingService.recordCancellationRefund` snapshots that amount right after cancellation (Server Action-level composition, since `booking` may not depend on `invoicing` — the reverse would be circular); a new `invoicing/refund-note-pdf.tsx` (mirrors `invoice-pdf.tsx`'s shape/letterhead/fonts) is generated inline and handed to the guest as a one-time base64 download on the confirmation screen (no separate download route exists for this later, since `lookupByBookingReference` deliberately treats a just-cancelled booking as a dead end) — staff can always regenerate the same PDF on demand from the booking detail page (new `streamRefundNotePdf` + `/api/v1/bookings/[bookingId]/refund-note-pdf` route) alongside a new inline display of the guest's reason/contact/tier/amount, right next to the existing "Mark Refunded" button, per explicit user request that staff must see the guest's own comment. (5) Insights' Finance section gains a "Pending Refunds" stat (`RevenueSummary.pendingRefunds`/`pendingRefundsCount`) — every `CANCELLED` booking with a snapshotted refund amount that staff hasn't yet marked `REFUNDED`, clearing the moment they do. (6) A real cookie-consent banner (`(guest)/CookieConsentBanner(Client).tsx`, a new `cookie_consent` cookie via `src/lib/set-cookie-consent-action.ts`) now gates the one non-essential cookie on the guest site — `wizard_session` (DR-155's plan-my-trip funnel analytics) — `recordWizardStepAction` is now a silent no-op without an explicit "accepted" choice; the auth session cookie and the `locale` preference cookie remain ungated (both strictly necessary). `SubmitButton` gains an optional `disabled` prop (ORed with its own `pending` state) to support the cancel form's checkbox-gated submit. No new module dependency in either direction: `booking`'s new method touches only `catalog` (already an existing dependency, to resolve a `PREDEFINED_PACKAGE` departure's `startDate`); `invoicing` already depends on `booking`; `insights` already depends on both. | V1, V6, V8 |
| DR-208 | 2026-08-31 | **Explicit user request.** The guest `/find-booking` result page's "Visa status" section showed a "Government fee: Requested/Paid" badge with no amount next to it -- `BookingLookupVisaView` (`visa/domain.ts`) only ever carried `status`/`feePaymentStatus`, unlike every other visa projection in the module (`VisaApplicationView`, `GuestVisaApplicationView`, `PendingVisaApplicationView`), which already expose `governmentFeeMinor`/`governmentFeeCurrency` (DR-187) -- a real, isolated gap in this one no-ctx guest-lookup view, not a new exposure decision (the sibling `GuestVisaApplicationView`'s own comment already documents this pair as "safe to expose to the guest -- just a fee amount + status, no PII"). Fixed by adding both fields to `BookingLookupVisaView`, populated in `visaService.getStatusForBookingLookup` from the same `VisaApplication` row already being read, and rendered inline next to the existing fee-status badge on `(guest)/find-booking/result/page.tsx` via the existing `format(money(...))` helper -- null on both exactly when the destination country has no public fee configured, same as the underlying columns. No schema/permission/module-dependency change. | V1, V6 |
| DR-209 | 2026-09-01 | **Explicit user request.** `/staff/visa-queue`'s Actions column had grown five stacked forms (contact, request documents, decide, upload document) with no visual separation -- restructured, same underlying actions, no new ones: the Document table column now shows the "Missing"/"On file" badge alongside its own upload/replace control (previously buried in Actions, disconnected from the status it drives), and Actions itself groups "Message tour lead" and "Decision" behind a `<details>`/`<summary>` disclosure each (pure CSS/HTML, no client JS) so a row's height reflects only what's currently relevant -- "Request documents" still shows only while the Document column reads Missing, unchanged from before. The message field is now a `<textarea>` (was a one-line `<input>`) that grows from `min-h-[3.5rem]` to `min-h-[7rem]` on focus (`SubmitButton` gains a passthrough `className` prop to support the stacked-panel layout). **Behavior change:** `visaService.contactTraveler` no longer routes through `notify()`'s WhatsApp -> SMS -> email fallback chain -- it now always sends via `notificationsService.notifyEmail` (Resend, `RESEND_FROM_EMAIL="Mufasa Safaris & Tours <info@mufasasafaris.com>"`, DR-205) straight to the tour lead's own on-file email, resolved the same way `bookingService.cancelForBookingLookup` (DR-207) already does (tour-lead `Traveler.email`, falling back to `Booking.contactEmail`, falling back once more here to the touristUserId's own non-nullable `User.email` so this can never hard-fail just because neither of the two more-specific fields was ever collected). `contactTravelerAction` (`visa-queue/actions.ts`) now also parses through `ContactTravelerInput.parse` (was reading `FormData` unvalidated) so the 1000-char cap is enforced server-side, not just by the textarea's `maxLength`. No schema/permission/module-dependency change -- `visa` already depended on `booking`/`notifications`/`auth`. | V1, V6, V8 |
| DR-210 | 2026-09-01 | **Explicit user request.** `/staff/visa-queue`'s Document column badge (Missing/On file) was purely derived from `VisaApplication.documentId !== null` -- no way to reflect a document handled outside the system (in person, a separate email thread) or to mark one not required for a given country/traveler, and no way to undo a wrong upload. New `VisaDocumentStatus` enum (`MISSING` / `RECEIVED` / `NOT_REQUIRED`) + `VisaApplication.documentStatus` column (`@default(MISSING)`), a facilitator-set flag deliberately independent of `documentId` -- same "staff-toggled, not system-derived" shape `VisaFeePaymentStatus`/DR-184 already established (any direction is valid at any time here, unlike that pair's forward-only transitions, since correcting a wrong pick should always be possible). New `visaService.updateDocumentStatus` (own `visa.document_status_updated` audit action) + `updateDocumentStatusAction`/`UpdateDocumentStatusInput`, surfaced as a `<select>` + Update button right under the badge in the Document column. `hasDocument` (tied to the real file) is unchanged and still gates a new "View" link to the granted document (`GET /api/v1/bookings/[bookingId]/travelers/[travelerId]/visa/document`, an existing route that was never actually linked from this page before) -- `documentStatus` is what "Request documents" on Actions and the "missing documents" stat card gate on now, not `hasDocument`. `npx prisma generate` was run to pick up the schema change; the actual `db push` against the shared Neon DB + `documentId`/passport backfill implications need the user to run it (see CLAUDE.md's "schema changes applied by hand" gotcha) before this is live. | V1, V4, V6 |
| DR-211 | 2026-09-01 | **Explicit user request**, raised mid-conversation ("request document and message tour leader tend to do the same process"). `/staff/visa-queue`'s "Request documents" was its own server-action form sending a fixed canned notification through `notify()`'s WhatsApp -> SMS -> email fallback chain -- a second, parallel path to "Message tour lead" (DR-209's forced-Resend-email free-text box) for what's fundamentally the same operation (ask the tour lead for something). Collapsed into one path: "Request documents" is now `PrefillMessageButton` (new client component, `visa-queue/PrefillMessageButton.tsx` -- the page's only bit of client JS; everywhere else stays plain server-action forms), a one-click shortcut that opens the Message panel and pre-fills its textarea with a canned, country-specific sentence (`StaffVisaQueue.requestDocumentsPrefill`, interpolates `{country}`) via a scoped DOM query (`closest('[data-visa-actions-panel]')`, keeping each table row's `<details>`/`<textarea>` pair isolated from every other row's) -- the facilitator can send it as-is or edit it, and either way it goes out through the exact same `contactTravelerAction` -> `notificationsService.notifyEmail` path DR-209 established. The now-unused `requestMissingDocumentsAction` wrapper in `visa-queue/actions.ts` was removed. **Deliberately left in place:** `visaService.requestMissingDocuments` itself, its dedicated `POST /api/v1/bookings/[bookingId]/travelers/[travelerId]/visa/request-documents` route, the `VISA_MISSING_DOCUMENTS` notification event, and their existing test coverage (`tests/api/visa-contact.api.test.ts`) -- this DR only changes which button on this one staff page triggers a tour-lead notification, not the underlying public API surface, which may still have other callers. No schema/permission/module-dependency change. | V1, V6 |
| DR-212 | 2026-09-01 | **Explicit user request, deliberately reverses part of DR-034's original scoping** ("visa status only surfaces when the finalized add-ons included Visa Assistance in the first place -- never a bare country-regulation dump," `(guest)/find-booking/result/page.tsx`'s own long-standing comment). The guest `/find-booking` lookup's "Trip status" card now also shows a brief, purely informational "Visa requirements" note -- the destination's `CountryRegulation.visaRequirements` staff-authored summary paragraph, plus (when travelers exist) a line listing the distinct nationalities in the party -- **regardless of whether Visa Assistance was purchased as an add-on**, unlike the existing per-traveler "Visa status" block right above it (which still only shows once, since that's the only case a `VisaApplication` row exists to track at all; the two are visually adjacent but functionally distinct now, per explicit user direction to put the new one "under visa status"). New no-ctx `immigrationService.getPublicVisaRequirements(country)` (`CountryRegulationPublicVisaInfo`, mirrors `getPublicFee`'s DR-184 shape -- just the one field, never embassy contact/health/advisories/fee) resolves null (never throws) when no regulation is on file, same graceful-degradation convention as every other public read in this module. Destination country resolution reuses the page's existing precedent (`tripSummary?.country` for `PREDEFINED_PACKAGE`, else `booking.customCountry` for `TAILOR_MADE`); nationalities are every distinct non-null `Traveler.nationality` already present on the `travelers` array this page already fetches (no new query, no new PII exposure beyond what DR-207's guest-lookup precedent already established fetching the full manifest for). No schema/permission/module-dependency change -- `immigration` already had zero inbound dependencies to speak of; this is a page-level import, same as every other module this page already composes directly. | V1, V6 |
| DR-213 | 2026-09-01 | **Explicit user request.** `/staff/visa-queue`'s Document column "View" link (added DR-210) carried no indication of what the document actually was -- just the bare word "View." New `VisaApplication.documentFileName` column (nullable `varchar(255)`), the original uploaded filename, snapshotted alongside `documentId` whenever a facilitator uploads the granted visa document (`visaService.uploadDocument`'s `UploadVisaDocumentInput` gains a required `fileName` field, sourced from the browser `File.name` at both of its call sites -- the visa-queue Server Action and the `POST /api/v1/bookings/[bookingId]/travelers/[travelerId]/visa/document` route) and cleared alongside `documentId` on resubmit (`visaRepository.resubmit`, same "stale doc reference" reasoning that clearing already had). Deliberately snapshotted onto `VisaApplication` itself rather than adding a filename column to the generic, multi-purpose `documents` table -- keeps the change scoped to the one place it's needed instead of a cross-cutting `Document` schema change affecting passport/fleet-compliance/guide-certification uploads too. | DR-214 | 2026-09-01 | **Explicit user request** ("let's make everything under Footer legal line editable"), following up on DR-204's narrower scope (only the "Cyber PolCo" link text/href were staff-editable; the year and "Mufasa Safaris & Tours" stayed hardcoded in `footer.tsx`). The guest footer's closing copyright line is now a fully staff-editable **template**, not three fixed segments plus one editable link: `CmsTextBlock` (key='footer.legal') repurposes its existing `eyebrow` column to hold the line template (e.g. the new default, `"© {year} Mufasa Safaris & Tours, a {link} Product."`), while `title`/`body` keep DR-204's link-label/href roles unchanged. Two live placeholders, `{year}` and `{link}`, are substituted at render time by a new `renderFooterLegalLine` in `footer.tsx` -- `{year}` is never persisted (always `new Date().getFullYear()`, per explicit user confirmation that the year must stay auto-updating rather than becoming a value staff types and can let go stale) and `{link}` becomes the real `<a>` element built from `title`/`body`, both as plain React text nodes (no `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`, so a staff typo can't inject markup). A blank template saved from `/staff/cms` is stored as `null` and falls back to the same default sentence, same "degrade to a placeholder" convention as every other CMS-backed guest list on this page (partners, social links, operating-countries map). `updateFooterLegalAction` (`staff/cms/actions.ts`) gained its own `FooterLegalTemplate` zod schema requiring the saved template to actually contain the literal `{link}` token, so a staff edit can't accidentally make the link vanish from the rendered line; `/staff/cms`'s "Footer legal line" tab now shows all three `PageTextEditor` fields (`showEyebrow` was previously forced `false` for this tab) with updated EN/FR copy explaining the two placeholders. Deliberately scoped as a one-line exception to DR-168's "brand name is fixed everywhere" rule, not a reversal of it -- every other guest-facing occurrence of "Mufasa Safaris & Tours" (header, hero, page titles, metadata) stays hardcoded exactly as DR-168 set it; only this one footer line's wording, including its brand-name text, is now staff's to edit if they choose. No schema/permission/module-dependency change (still the same `CmsTextBlock` row DR-204 already created). | V1, V6 |

| DR-215 | 2026-09-02 | **Explicit user request** ("let's use resend even tho DPO isn't fully wired"), following an investigation into which email fires when a guest finishes checkout. Real bug found: `PAYMENT_SUCCEEDED` (fired by `invoicing/service.ts`'s `applyPaymentOutcome` the moment `initiatePayment` auto-succeeds, DR-074) went through `notify()`'s WhatsApp -> SMS -> EMAIL fallback chain, which resolves EMAIL from the recipient's `User.email` -- for the guest checkout flow (no tourist accounts, ever; better-auth's `anonymous` plugin), that's a synthetic, undeliverable placeholder (`temp@<id>.com`, the plugin's default with no `emailDomainName` configured), not a real address. With WhatsApp unconfigured (OI-06) and SMS on a low account balance (OI-07), this confirmation was often not reaching the guest on any channel at all. Fixed by adding `notifyPaymentSucceeded` (invoicing/service.ts), which resolves the real recipient the same way `bookingService.cancelForBookingLookup` (DR-207) and `visaService.contactTraveler` (DR-209) already do -- tour lead `Traveler.email` -> `Booking.contactEmail` -> `User.email` as a last resort -- then sends straight over EMAIL via Resend (`notificationsService.notifyEmail`), bypassing the fallback chain entirely for this event; `PAYMENT_FAILED` is unchanged, still on `notify()`. New `invoicing -> auth` runtime dependency (`authService.getUser`, for locale + the last-resort email) -- confirmed acyclic, same shape DR-205 already established for `visa -> auth`; `auth` never imports `invoicing`. The whole new function is wrapped in try/catch and never throws (extends `notify()`/`notifyEmail()`'s own never-throws contract one layer up) since the payment already committed before this runs -- a notification-composition failure must never turn an already-successful payment into a 500 for the guest. Also (explicit user request, richer email body): `PAYMENT_SUCCEEDED`'s HTML template is no longer a one-sentence receipt -- it now renders a details block (booking reference, trip title/country, travel dates, traveler count, amount) inside the existing branded shell, via a new `summaryTable` helper in `notifications/domain.ts`, and distinguishes a `DEPOSIT` payment ("your spot is on hold, balance still due") from `BALANCE`/`FULL` ("fully paid and confirmed") via a new `NotificationData.paymentKind` field -- same distinction reflected in the plain-text SMS template (still used only by the rare no-email-found fallback path). `NotificationData` gained `tripTitle`/`tripCountry`/`paymentKind` (new) and reuses `seats`/`travelStart`/`travelEnd` (previously TAILOR_MADE_REQUEST_RECEIVED-only). `NotificationData` itself is now exported from `notifications/index.ts` (previously only `NotificationEvent` was) so a calling module can type the object it builds. No schema/permission change. | V1, V6, V9 |

| DR-216 | 2026-09-02 | **User-reported bug**: a guest hit an error uploading a passport PDF on mobile the day before. Investigated the guest passport-upload flow ((guest)/booking/[bookingId]/passport/actions.ts's `uploadPassportAction`, a Server Action, not a route) and found this is a recurrence of the exact DR-182 incident, on a different Server Action: Next.js's own `experimental.serverActions.bodySizeLimit` (`next.config.mjs`) rejects the request in its own request-parsing layer, before the action's code -- and therefore its own `documents/domain.ts` `isValidDocumentUpload` check (which already allows up to `MAX_PASSPORT_SIZE_BYTES`, 10MB) -- ever runs. A mobile scan/camera-to-PDF app routinely produces a 5-15MB file for even a 1-2 page passport, well past the limit. Fixed the same way DR-182 did: raised the shared, app-wide `bodySizeLimit` from `4mb` to `4.5mb`, the practical maximum given Vercel's own hard ~4.5MB platform ceiling for a serverless function's request body (a ceiling this setting can't be configured past, per the DR-182/DR-163 comments already on this config). **Explicit user choice**, offered as an alternative to the durable fix: this only raises the effective cap by ~0.5MB and does not close the gap with the documented 10MB passport allowance -- a passport PDF between ~4.5MB and 10MB will still fail exactly as before. The durable fix (moving passport upload to the same direct-to-Blob client-upload pattern DR-163 already uses for video, which bypasses this platform ceiling entirely) was declined for now; both `next.config.mjs`'s comment and this entry flag it as the real remaining gap if this recurs again. No schema/permission/module-dependency change. | V1, V6 |
| DR-217 | 2026-09-02 | **Explicit user request** ("I want these email templates to be added under cms"), scoped via two clarifying questions: copy-only editing (eyebrow/heading/body text; layout, colors, logo, subject line, and CTA buttons stay code-driven) across all 26 `NotificationEvent` kinds. Reuses the existing `cms` module's `CmsTextBlock` table rather than a new one -- its `key`/`locale`/`title`/`eyebrow`/`body` shape already matches exactly what an email needs (key `email.<TEMPLATE_KEY>`, `title` -> heading, `body` -> the editable prose) -- so no schema migration. `PAYMENT_SUCCEEDED` is the one event whose default copy genuinely branches into two different sentences (deposit vs. full payment, DR-215), not just an optional word or fallback value; split into two independently-editable template keys, `PAYMENT_SUCCEEDED_DEPOSIT`/`_FULL`, so a staff edit can't collapse that distinction -- every other event keeps a 1:1 key. `notifications/domain.ts` (kept pure, no DB/framework import) gained `EMAIL_TEMPLATE_DEFAULTS`/`EMAIL_TEMPLATE_TOKENS`/`EMAIL_TEMPLATE_GROUPS` (the coded EN/FR default per key, its `{{token}}` names, and its staff-UI grouping) plus `resolveContent`/`applyBodyTemplate`/`escapeHtml`: a staff override (or the coded default when none exists) is escaped as plain text first, `{{token}}` placeholders are substituted with pre-formatted, equally-escaped values (booking reference, amount, traveler name, etc. -- computed exactly the same way whether or not an override is in play), and newlines become `<br>` -- the same "plain text, not HTML" contract every other `CmsTextBlock.body` already has on the guest site. An unresolved `{{token}}` (e.g. a typo) is left as literal text rather than silently dropped, so a mistake is visible in the sent email. Any event with fixed structured/tabular content (`PAYMENT_SUCCEEDED`'s `summaryTable` details block) keeps that part entirely code-driven, appended after the editable prose. `renderMessage` gained a 4th `overrides` parameter (default `{}`, fully backward compatible) rather than reaching into `cms` itself. New runtime dependency **`notifications` -> `cms`** (`notifications/service.ts`'s new `getEmailOverrides`, one bulk read per send via a new `cmsService.listPublicTextBlocksByKeyPrefix('email.', locale)` -- one query for all 27 keys, not 27) -- confirmed acyclic: `cms` has zero inbound module dependencies today and imports nothing from `notifications`. A CMS read failure degrades to the coded defaults (never blocks a transactional email, charter rule 8), verified directly in this sandbox via a real Neon connectivity drop. New staff surface: `/staff/cms`'s "Emails" tab (27 collapsible editors grouped by Booking/Payment/Visa/Ratings/Trip planning/Staff accounts/Staff assignments), reusing the existing `PageTextEditor` component and `updatePageTextAction` verbatim -- no new Server Action. Prefilled with the coded default (not blank) via a new `withEmailFallback`, same "start from what's actually live" convention DR-207's `withTermsFallback` established for the Terms tab, sourced from `EMAIL_TEMPLATE_DEFAULTS` instead of a messages/*.json default. No new permission (`cms.read`/`cms.write`, SUPERADMIN-only, already gates this page). Same session, explicit user request, no schema/permission/module-dependency change (bundled here rather than as separate DRs since neither is structural): the email shell (`email-template.ts`) now links Google Fonts' CSS2 endpoint for the site's three Horizon typefaces (Big Shoulders Stencil Display/Archivo/Special Elite) with the prior Arial/Helvetica stack kept as the fallback for clients (most of them) that ignore the stylesheet link; every email footer gained a "Powered by www.cyberpolco.com" line; and `global-error.tsx` (the root-layout crash fallback) now loads the same three fonts directly via `next/font`, fixing a latent bug where its Tailwind `font-mono`/`font-display` classes referenced CSS variables that were never actually defined on that page. | V1, V6, V8 |
| DR-218 | 2026-09-02 | **Explicit user request**: Botswana added as this app's 5th operating country (Namibia, DRC, Zambia, Zimbabwe, now Botswana), scoped via clarifying questions into three parts. (1) Core booking eligibility: `OPERATING_COUNTRY_CODES` (`src/lib/country-codes.ts`) gains `'BW'` -- the single shared source `catalog/domain.ts`'s `CreatePackageInput`/`UpdatePackageInput` country/countries validation, `cms/domain.ts`'s `GALLERY_COUNTRY_CODES`, and every guest/staff country picker (packages filter, plan-my-trip destination step x2, staff tailor-made-booking form, weather country grouping) already read from -- so all of these picked up Botswana with no further code change once the constant changed. Three call sites that had each redeclared the same 4-country literal ad hoc before this constant existed (`bookings/[bookingId]/page.tsx`, `travelers/new/page.tsx`, `find-booking/result/page.tsx`) were refactored to import the shared constant instead of a 5th hand-copy. `provinces.ts`'s `SITE_COUNTRIES`/`PROVINCES_BY_COUNTRY` (a deliberately narrower list scoped to itinerary Sites only, per its own explicit-user-direction comment) also gains Botswana -- its 10 administrative districts plus Gaborone and Francistown (its 2 city-status districts), same "district + provincial-status city" shape as Zimbabwe's existing entry, deliberately omitting Botswana's smaller town councils. `weather-towns.ts` gains 3 Botswana towns (Gaborone, Maun, Kasane) for the guest `/weather` page. `Countries` i18n namespace (`en.json`/`fr.json`) gains a `BW` entry. (2) Homepage decorative map: `(guest)/page.tsx`'s `FALLBACK_OPERATING_COUNTRIES` (DR-202's staff-configurable-with-a-coded-fallback list) gains a 5th entry (capital/languages/currency/population/area) -- the homepage map itself (`AfricaMap.tsx`) needed no code change, since DR-202 already sources from the full 55-country AU list and Botswana (`BW`) was already in it. The `regional-compliance` skill doc gains a Botswana operator-compliance/visa/no-BR-07-zone section, researched from public sources (Botswana e-Visa portal, Botswana Tourism Organisation, gov.bw Tourism Enterprise Application, PwC/AfroTools/TaxAtlas VAT summaries) and explicitly flagged not verified against the Department of Immigration and Citizenship or the Department of Tourism directly. (3) Regulatory seed content, per explicit user direction to research it: `prisma/seed.ts` gains a Botswana `CountryRegulation` row (visa exemption for most Commonwealth/EU/US/SADC nationalities up to 90 days, e-Visa for the rest, KAZA UniVisa cross-border note, Okavango/Chobe malaria note, no active-conflict security-zone concern unlike DRC/BR-07) and a Botswana `TaxRate` row (14% VAT, `rateBp: 1400`) -- both explicitly commented "estimate, verify" per this project's existing DR-034 convention for every seeded tax/visa figure, not yet applied to the live shared Neon DB in this session (seed data changes go through the same by-hand `db:seed`-against-the-real-DB process as any other schema/data change, confirmed with the user first). No Prisma schema change: every country field this touches (`TourPackage.country`/`countries`, `Hotel`/`Restaurant`/`Site.country`, `TaxRate.country`, `CountryRegulation.country`) is already a plain `String` column with app-layer (not DB-enum) validation, so this is a pure code + seed-data change, no migration/`db:push`/`db:rls` needed. Demo seed packages/hotels/restaurants for Botswana were explicitly scoped out for this round. | V1, V3, V6, V9, V11 |
| DR-219 | 2026-09-02 | **Explicit user request**: "Instead of Departure date: add (estimated) when booking or tailor made, and those dates even when confirmed can be changed only by tour operator or superadmin." Two parts. (1) Labeling: every place a booking's trip date is displayed to a guest or staff user now reads "(estimated)"/"(à titre indicatif)" alongside it -- `FindBookingResult.travelDates` (guest lookup, both origins), the TAILOR_MADE pill and PREDEFINED_PACKAGE trip-summary line on `staff/bookings/[bookingId]/page.tsx`, the trip-dates pill on `staff/itineraries/[itineraryId]/page.tsx`, and `itinerary-summary-pdf.tsx`'s "Travel dates" line. New shared `Common.estimatedSuffix` key (`en.json`/`fr.json`) -- French uses the invariant "(à titre indicatif)" rather than a gendered/numbered "(estimé/estimée/…)" that would need to agree differently at each call site. (2) A booking's trip date (`Departure.startDate` for PREDEFINED_PACKAGE, `Booking.customTravelStart`/`customTravelEnd` for a not-yet-converted TAILOR_MADE request) was previously not editable at all after creation -- now editable, but restricted to SUPERADMIN/TOUR_OPERATOR at any status short of the terminal ones `isBookingLocked` already blocks (not just post-confirmation -- the literal request read as an absolute restriction, not one that loosens before confirmation). New `booking/domain.ts` `isDepartureDateChanger` (identical role set to the existing `isBookingConfirmer`, kept as its own named function per this module's per-action-helper convention) gates the new `bookingService.updateTripDates`, which routes a PREDEFINED_PACKAGE (or already-converted TAILOR_MADE, DR-028) booking's date change to a new `catalogService.updateDepartureDate` (recomputes `endDate` from the package's `durationDays`, or preserves a bespoke departure's own day span) and a still-unconverted TAILOR_MADE request's date change straight to a new `bookingRepository.updateTravelDates`. Refuses outright (409) when the target Departure is shared with another live booking (a real, expected case per DR-082's own "a departure can have several bookings" note) -- rescheduling would silently move another guest's trip too, which needs its own dedicated flow, not this one. New `PATCH /api/v1/bookings/[bookingId]/trip-dates` route (permission `booking.confirm`, same base permission `confirm`'s own route uses -- PLATFORM_ADMIN holds it too but is rejected by `isDepartureDateChanger`, same two-layer "route passes the broader permission, the service narrows it" convention as `isBookingConfirmer`) plus a matching `updateTripDatesAction` Server Action and a staff-facing date-change form on the booking detail page (hardcoded to `SUPERADMIN`/`TOUR_OPERATOR` in the page itself, matching the Confirm button's own existing inline role check rather than a new import). No schema change (`Departure.startDate`/`endDate` and `Booking.customTravelStart`/`customTravelEnd` already existed and were already mutable Prisma columns, just previously unreached by any service method) and no new `Permission` (reuses `booking.confirm`/`catalog.write`). Unit-tested (`isDepartureDateChanger` in `tests/booking.domain.test.ts`) and API-tested (`tests/api/booking-trip-dates.security.test.ts`, asserting PLATFORM_ADMIN gets 403 and TOUR_OPERATOR/SUPERADMIN get 200 on a CONFIRMED booking) -- the API test could not be run to completion in this session due to a transient sandbox-to-Neon connectivity failure (reproduced with a bare Prisma script too, so not vitest-specific this time; `npm run typecheck`/`npm run lint` both pass clean and the pure-domain test suite passes). | V1, V4, V6 |
| DR-220 | 2026-09-03 | **Explicit user request**: "now deposit is 30% let's apply changes everywhere possible." Business-rule change to the DR-012 deposit/balance split: 40%/60% -> 30%/70%, post-tax/post-fee total, same half-up rounding with balance absorbing any remainder (unchanged mechanics, only the ratio literal moved). Sole code change: `invoicing/domain.ts`'s `splitDeposit` (`0.4` -> `0.3`); every consumer (`computeInvoiceAmounts`, `canInitiatePayment`, invoice/PDF rendering, `amountForPaymentKind`) derives from that one function, so nothing else in the runtime path needed touching. Every hardcoded duplicate of the old 40%/60% figure was updated to keep prose/UI/docs/tests consistent with the new rule: guest-facing i18n copy in `en.json`/`fr.json` (`depositPct`/`balancePct` labels in both `PackageBooking`/`FindBooking` namespaces, the Terms of Service payment paragraph, and the Cancellation & Refund Policy deposit paragraph, EN+FR); `prisma/seed.ts`'s "How do I pay?" FAQ entry; `docs/openapi.yaml`'s `depositMinor` field description; and the hardcoded-ratio assertions in `tests/invoicing.domain.test.ts` (`splitDeposit`, `computeInvoiceAmounts`, `computeCancellationRefundAmountMinor` fixtures), `tests/api/invoices.api.test.ts` (its own duplicated `0.4` in `expectedAmounts`, plus fixture/assertion literals), and `tests/lib/coupons.test.ts`'s fixture invoice. Does not touch: the Cancellation & Refund Policy's tiered refund percentages (60+/30-59/14-29/under-14-day bands, an unrelated DR-207 concept unaffected by this change) or any `*Bp`/basis-point rate table (`TaxRate`/`PlatformRate`/`LateBookingRate`) -- the deposit ratio is a plain-fraction literal in code, not a settings-module row, so no new admin UI or migration is implied by this change. | V1, V6, V7 |
| DR-221 | 2026-09-03 | **User-reported bug, then explicit user follow-up request**: creating a staff account with 2+ roles at once could silently drop every role but the first. **Root cause**: `authService.createUser` wrote `User.role` (via `finalizeAdminCreatedUser`) and every `Membership` row (via a separate `createMemberships`) as two independent, non-transactional statements -- `finalizeAdminCreatedUser` had already been hardened (prior session) to retry Prisma's `P2025` (a suspected Neon pooler read-after-write visibility lag right after `auth.api.signUpEmail` resolves), but `createMemberships` right after it had no such retry and no dedicated error handling in `createUserAction`'s catch, so a transient failure there left the account holding only its primary role with zero real `Membership` rows and no visible error path back to the admin. **Fix**: folded both writes into one `finalizeAdminCreatedUser(userId, { roles, phone, organizationId })`, run inside a single `withOrg` transaction (matching the atomicity `replaceRoles`/DR-035's edit path already had) and retried as a whole on `P2025`, same backoff as before; `createMemberships` is removed, no longer a separate step. **Role concurrency, explicitly reviewed pair-by-pair** (all 21 combinations of the 7 `ASSIGNABLE_ROLES`, walked through and confirmed/ruled out one at a time, not inferred -- three initial statements produced contradictory results for (SUPERADMIN, VEHICLE_OWNER), (SUPERADMIN, VISA_FACILITATOR) and (VEHICLE_OWNER, VISA_FACILITATOR), each resolved by explicit follow-up confirmation): SUPERADMIN is compatible only with VISA_FACILITATOR; PLATFORM_ADMIN is compatible only with TOUR_OPERATOR; TOUR_OPERATOR is compatible with everything except SUPERADMIN; TOUR_GUIDE/DRIVER/VEHICLE_OWNER are compatible with each other and TOUR_OPERATOR only; VISA_FACILITATOR is compatible only with SUPERADMIN/TOUR_OPERATOR. New pure `auth/domain.ts` `ROLE_COMPATIBILITY` map + `findIncompatibleRolePair(roles)` (now also exported through `auth/index.ts`), wired into `CreateUserInput`/`UpdateUserInput` via `.superRefine` -- the actual gate, enforced identically whether the request comes from the staff Server Actions (`admin/users/actions.ts`) or the REST routes (`api/v1/users`, `api/v1/users/[userId]`), since both parse the same shared schema. UX-only client-side mirror: a new shared `RoleCheckboxGroup` (replaces the inline checkbox grid duplicated in `create-user-form.tsx`/`edit-user-form.tsx`) calls the same `findIncompatibleRolePair` live as boxes are checked, shows an inline error, and disables the submit button while a conflict exists -- charter rule 1, the real enforcement is server-side regardless of what this shows. Verified against `prisma/seed.ts` directly that no seeded account (the bootstrap SUPERADMIN included) actually holds a Membership set this rule would now reject -- CLAUDE.md's "(PLATFORM_ADMIN + own-org TOUR_OPERATOR)" aside was describing what SUPERADMIN's wildcard already grants permission-wise, not a literal second/third `Membership` row; the real seed writes one `SUPERADMIN` row only. Whether any *already-created* production account (created via the admin UI before this rule existed, not via seed) holds a now-incompatible combination was not checked in this session (no DB access) -- flagged as an open item below, since such an account's roles couldn't be re-saved unchanged through the edit form until fixed. No schema change, no new `Permission`, no new module dependency. | V1, V6, V8 |
| DR-222 | 2026-09-03 | **Explicit user request**: add two new guest-facing add-on types, FLIGHT_TICKET and ESIM, priced by a guest-picked variant instead of a flat per-country rate. New `AddonCode` values `ESIM`/`FLIGHT_TICKET`; new `FlightClass` enum (`ECONOMY`/`BUSINESS`/`FIRST`). Two new platform-wide reference tables in the `finance` module, same "no organizationId, no RLS" precedent as `AddonRate`/`TaxRate`: `Airport` (a small staff-curated list -- iataCode/name/city/country/active -- giving `FlightFareRate` a real FK-able route identity, not an exhaustive world database) and `FlightFareRate` (origin/destination `Airport` FKs + free-text `airline` + `flightClass`, effective-dated like every other Operational Rate -- a genuinely multi-dimensional rate, unlike `AddonRate`'s small fixed enum, so it needs a real per-row identity the same way `HotelRate`/`ActivityFee` do) and `EsimDataPlanRate` (`country` + `dataAllowanceGb` as a compound key, otherwise `AddonRate`'s exact auto-resolved/no-id/effective-dated shape). `BookingAddon` gains 5 nullable snapshot columns -- `flightClass`/`airline`/`originAirportCode`/`destinationAirportCode` (FLIGHT_TICKET) and `dataAllowanceGb` (ESIM) -- populated together per addon code, same "snapshot the resolved fact, don't just FK it" convention as `Invoice.lateBookingSurchargeRateBp`, so a later `Airport` edit/deactivation never mutates an already-selected add-on. **`booking` module**: `SetAddonsInput`'s shape changed from a flat `{ addonServiceIds: string[] }` to `{ addons: AddonSelectionInput[] }`, each selection carrying `addonServiceId` plus the 5 variant fields as optional -- a breaking wire-format change, so every existing caller (the guest and staff add-ons Server Actions, and the API/security test suites) was updated in the same change, not left on the old shape. `bookingService.setAddons` resolves FLIGHT_TICKET's price via a new `src/lib/flight-fare-rate.ts` (`getEffectiveFlightFareRate`, route+airline+class+effective-date, mirroring `src/lib/addon-rates.ts`'s existing no-`AuthContext` public-read shape) and ESIM's via a new `src/lib/esim-rate.ts` (`getEffectiveEsimRate`, country+GB-tier+effective-date) -- both plain-function lib helpers, not a `booking -> finance` module dependency (same precedent as the existing `booking -> addon-rates.ts` relationship), confirmed acyclic. FLIGHT_TICKET requires all 4 of `originAirportId`/`destinationAirportId`/`airline`/`flightClass`; ESIM requires `dataAllowanceGb`; every other existing `AddonCode` now explicitly rejects a selection carrying any of the 5 variant fields (previously unreachable, now a real validation rule). `BookingAddonView` also gains `code`/`name` (joined from `AddonService` at read time -- previously missing entirely, a pre-existing gap this closes as a side effect). **`finance` module**: full CRUD for all three new tables (`list/create/update/deleteAirport`, `...FlightFareRate`, `...EsimDataPlanRate`), reads gated by `finance_config.read`, writes by the existing `requireRateWriter` (SUPERADMIN-only, same as every other rate table), each write audited (`finance.airport_created`/etc.); explicitly does NOT trigger `reapplyRatesToAllCostBreakdowns` on update, same reasoning as `AddonRate`'s own update action -- these are resolved live at add-on selection time, never snapshotted into a cost breakdown. Three new public, no-ctx reads (`listPublicAirports`/`listPublicFlightFareOptions`/`listPublicEsimPlans`) back the guest picker, mirroring `cms`/`immigration`'s existing public-read convention. **UI**: new staff CRUD pages `/staff/finance/rates/flights` (Airports + FlightFareRates) and `/staff/finance/rates/esim` (EsimDataPlanRate), linked as two more cards from the existing Finance hub, gated identically to `/staff/finance/rates`. Both the guest (`(guest)/booking/[bookingId]/addons`) and staff (`staff/bookings/[bookingId]/addons`) add-on-selection pages previously resolved every add-on's display price via `getEffectiveAddonRate`, which always returns null for FLIGHT_TICKET/ESIM (they aren't priced via `AddonRate`) -- both pages now split add-ons into flat-priced (unchanged path) and variant-priced, rendering a new shared cascading picker (`FlightTicketPicker`: Origin -> Destination -> Airline -> Class, filtered at each step to only combinations that actually exist among currently-effective rates) and a flat plan-tier picker (`EsimPlanPicker`) below the existing checkbox list. A variant selection is submitted as a hidden, JSON-encoded `flightSelection`/`esimSelection` form field per pick (a plain checkbox can't carry structured per-selection data), merged server-side with the plain `addonServiceId` checkboxes into one `{ addons: [...] }` array before validating -- same convention in both the guest and staff Server Actions. Full EN/FR i18n parity for every new label/picker/error. Schema pushed to the shared Neon database (`npm run db:push`) using the user's own pasted `neondb_owner` credential, per this repo's by-hand-with-confirmation convention -- verified against a read-only `\d` on all three new tables plus the widened `booking_addons`, and `npm run db:rls` reapplied clean (150 statements) afterward to confirm no policy regression. Before running the push, the pasted credential was independently verified via `psql` to actually be this project's database (matching schema/tables), after a first pasted credential turned out to point at an entirely unrelated project (a different app's schema -- academy/jobs/starlink-support tables, no `organizations`/`bookings` at all) and was rejected without being touched. No new RLS policy needed (all three new tables are platform-wide, same as `AddonRate`). | V1, V6, V7 |
| DR-223 | 2026-09-03 | **User-reported bug ("I've generated a rating code again but the client/tour lead never received anything"), then explicit user follow-up planning session on channel routing across every `NotificationEvent`.** Root cause of the reported bug: `ratingsService.issueRatingCode`'s primary send (`notify('RATING_CODE_ISSUED', booking.touristUserId, ...)`) resolves the recipient's email from `User.email`, which for an anonymous-checkout guest is a synthetic, undeliverable placeholder (`temp@<id>.com`) -- the exact class of gap DR-215/DR-209/DR-207 already fixed elsewhere. `issueRatingCode`'s own DR-205 "guaranteed copy" safety net only checked `Booking.contactEmail`, which is **only ever populated for a TAILOR_MADE booking** (DR-047) -- for the far more common PREDEFINED_PACKAGE booking it's always `null`, so that safety net silently never fired. Compounded by `RATING_CODE_ISSUED` having no SMS/WhatsApp template at all (confirmed: not a deliberate email-only decision like `PAYMENT_SUCCEEDED`/DR-215, just an oversight), so email was the only channel that could ever deliver it. **Fix**: `issueRatingCode` now resolves the recipient the same DR-194 way `notifyPaymentSucceeded`/`contactTraveler`/`cancelForBookingLookup` already do -- tour lead `Traveler.email` (via `bookingService.listTravelers`) -> `Booking.contactEmail` -> the tourist's own `User.email` as a last resort -- and sends once via `notifyEmail` to that resolved address, replacing the old two-attempt (one silently-wrong, one silently-skipped) shape. Confirmed with the user: `RATING_CODE_ISSUED` stays deliberately email-only (a short code is arguably SMS-friendly, but explicitly not pursued this round) -- only the recipient-resolution bug was in scope for this event. **Broader planning session**: walked the full 26-event `NotificationEvent` channel-routing map with the user (case-by-case, not a blanket policy) and confirmed/changed: `VISA_APPROVED`/`VISA_REJECTED` were accidentally email-only (no SMS template existed, unlike their sibling `VISA_SUBMITTED`/`VISA_RESUBMITTED`) -- explicit decision to add a short WhatsApp/SMS heads-up template ("check your email for details") sent **in parallel with, not as a fallback substitute for,** the full email (which still carries the real actionable content -- download link / rejection reason / resubmit CTA). New `notificationsService.notifyEmailWithHeadsUp(event, {email, phone}, locale, organizationId, data)` (`notifications/service.ts`) implements this: always sends the full email via the existing `notifyEmail`, then independently attempts WhatsApp-then-SMS for the short heads-up (best-effort, never blocks or is blocked by the email leg) -- a genuinely new send shape, distinct from `notify()`'s single stop-at-first-success fallback chain and from `notifyEmail`/`notifySms`'s single-channel-only shape. Takes an explicit `{email, phone}` recipient (not a `userId`) so the caller resolves the real contact first, same "explicit recipient" precedent as `notifyEmail`/`notifySms` (DR-055/DR-056). `visaService.decideApplication` now resolves the recipient via the same DR-194 chain (previously called bare `notify()` against `booking.touristUserId`, the identical synthetic-email gap `RATING_CODE_ISSUED` had) and calls `notifyEmailWithHeadsUp` for both `VISA_APPROVED` and `VISA_REJECTED`. **Explicitly left unchanged, decided case-by-case with the user, not assumed**: `VISA_MISSING_DOCUMENTS` and `VISA_QUEUE_NEW_APPLICATION` (staff-internal) stay email-only -- now a documented deliberate choice rather than an accidental gap, since both already had no SMS template; `PAYMENT_FAILED` stays on the plain fallback chain (not upgraded to a dual-send) -- explicit user call that stopping at first success is preferable to double-noticing the guest for this event; every other event's routing (14 more on the full WhatsApp->SMS->email chain, `PAYMENT_SUCCEEDED`/`VISA_CONTACT_TRAVELER`/`STAFF_PASSWORD_ISSUED`/`STAFF_PASSWORD_RESET` deliberately email-only per their own prior DRs, `TAILOR_MADE_REQUEST_RECEIVED`'s existing explicit-recipient special case) is untouched. No schema change, no new permission. Tests: `tests/notifications.channel-routing.test.ts` gains 3 new cases covering `notifyEmailWithHeadsUp`'s always-email/best-effort-heads-up/WhatsApp-then-SMS-fallback behavior, and its pre-existing "no plain-text template" case was moved from `VISA_APPROVED` (now has one) to `VISA_MISSING_DOCUMENTS` (still doesn't); `tests/notifications.domain.test.ts`'s `SMS_EVENTS` fixture and its own "no SMS template" case were updated the same way. | V1, V6, V8 |
| DR-224 | 2026-09-03 | **User-reported bug (production)**: creating a staff account with roles DRIVER+TOUR_GUIDE+TOUR_OPERATOR failed with a generic "Something went wrong" (`Errors.internal()`'s canned title). **DR-221 follow-up, same root-cause family, one step further down**: `authService.createUser`'s final re-fetch of the just-created account (`authRepository.findUserById(result.user.id)`, right after `finalizeAdminCreatedUser`'s and `audit()`'s writes) uses `prisma.user.findUnique`, which returns `null` on a Neon pooler read-after-write visibility miss rather than throwing `P2025` -- so unlike the two writes immediately before it, this read was never covered by `withTransientRetry`/`isTransientDbError` (both keyed on a *thrown* error) and a lag here surfaced as a bare `Errors.internal()` even though the account, with every selected role, had already committed. Not role-combo-specific -- confirmed via static trace that `ROLE_COMPATIBILITY` allows every pair in DRIVER/TOUR_GUIDE/TOUR_OPERATOR and that fleet auto-provisioning (`provisionFleetProfilesForUser`) treats TOUR_OPERATOR as a no-op, identical to the already-tested/working DRIVER+TOUR_GUIDE case -- intermittent timing, reproducible on any role combo including ones already working in production (two existing accounts already hold 5 simultaneous roles). **Fix**: `authService.createUser` now retries the re-fetch up to 3 additional times (150ms/attempt backoff, matching `withTransientRetry`'s cadence) when it comes back `null`, before giving up and throwing `Errors.internal()`. New regression test (`tests/api/users-admin.api.test.ts`, "creates a user with three simultaneous roles including TOUR_OPERATOR") closes the confirmed gap that no test exercised a 3-role combination including TOUR_OPERATOR. No schema change, no new permission, no module dependency change. | V6, V8 |
| DR-225 | 2026-09-03 | **User-reported bug batch (three unrelated issues, one session).** (1) `/staff/cms`'s Footer legal tab rendered blank fields whenever no `footer.legal` `CmsTextBlock` row existed yet -- `PageTextEditor`'s `current` prop was passed the raw (possibly-`null`) DB read directly, unlike every Terms section (`withTermsFallback`), which prefills from a coded default. Fixed: `footer.tsx`'s three fallback constants (`FALLBACK_FOOTER_LEGAL_TEMPLATE`/`_LABEL`/`_URL`) are now exported and `cms/page.tsx` builds a `footerLegalView` (same shape `withTermsFallback` returns) from them when no row exists, so the editor now shows the same template/label/URL the guest footer actually falls back to, not blank inputs. (2) Every downloadable PDF's "Operating in Namibia, DRC, Zambia & Zimbabwe" footer line (`finance/package-summary-pdf.tsx`, `invoicing/invoice-pdf.tsx`, `invoicing/refund-note-pdf.tsx`, `itinerary/itinerary-summary-pdf.tsx`, EN+FR where each file has a French string) missed **Botswana**, added DR-218 -- these four hardcoded strings were never updated when Botswana became a 5th operating country; now read "...Zambia, Zimbabwe & Botswana" / "...Zambie, au Zimbabwe et au Botswana". (3) **Real bug found**: `authService.listUsersByRole`/`authRepository.findUsersByRole` (added DR-205 to alert every `VISA_FACILITATOR` in the org of a new visa application) filtered only on `User.role` (the primary role), never on `Membership` rows -- the same class of gap DR-026's whole multi-role model exists to prevent. Since `ROLE_COMPATIBILITY` (DR-221) only ever pairs `VISA_FACILITATOR` with `SUPERADMIN`/`TOUR_OPERATOR`, a facilitator's primary `User.role` is routinely the *other* role in the pair, with `VISA_FACILITATOR` living only in `organization_members` -- meaning this lookup could silently return nobody, and the DR-205 facilitator alert would never fire, for any org where every facilitator holds the role as a secondary one. Fixed: `findUsersByRole` now unions primary-role matches with `Membership`-role matches (same "primary role falls back, Membership extends it" shape `resolveRoles` already uses per-user), deduped by user id. No schema change, no new permission, no module-dependency change; `tsc`/`lint`/`tests/notifications.domain.test.ts`/`tests/assignment.domain.test.ts`/`tests/auth.domain.test.ts` all still pass. A driver/guide/vehicle-owner departure-assignment notice (`ASSIGNMENT_NOTICE_DRIVER`/`_GUIDE`/`_VEHICLE_OWNER`, also DR-205) was investigated in the same session -- `assignmentService.createAssignment`'s notify() call sites were verified correct via a mocked reproduction (right recipient id/event/data for all three roles) -- no further bug found there this round. | V1, V6, V8, V9 |
| DR-226 | 2026-09-03 | **User-reported bug (production), same call site as DR-224 but a step earlier, and still failing after DR-224 shipped.** `finalizeAdminCreatedUser`'s `tx.user.update` (writing role/phone/organizationId/emailVerified/mustChangePassword right after `auth.api.signUpEmail` resolves) started failing with `P2025` "Record to update not found" **on every single admin create-user attempt, not occasionally** -- confirmed via `vercel logs`: 4 identical `prisma:error` lines per failed `POST /staff/admin/users`, exactly matching the existing `withTransientRetry` call's `maxAttempts=4`, with no corresponding `User` row ever landing in the database afterward (checked directly). This is the exact "Neon pooler read-after-write visibility lag" class already documented in this function's own comment ("the row shows up moments later ... it's just not yet visible to this immediate follow-up query") -- DR-224 fixed a *different*, later step in the same call chain (`findUserById`'s re-fetch) and didn't touch this one, so it kept failing after DR-224 deployed. Investigated and ruled out before landing on "needs more retry budget": role-combination-specific bug (ruled out -- the user's own report started as DRIVER+TOUR_GUIDE+TOUR_OPERATOR but escalated to "even one role" failing, and the failing call has no role-count sensitivity), RLS blocking the update (ruled out -- confirmed via `prisma/rls.sql` that `users` has no RLS policy at all, unlike every tenant table), a stale/dropped `getPrimaryOrgId()`/`isPrimary` flag leaving `organizationId` null on the created row (ruled out -- queried production directly, exactly one org, "Lam", has `isPrimary: true`), a `better-auth`/`@prisma/client` version drift (ruled out -- installed versions match `package.json`'s pins exactly), a real Neon regional outage (ruled out -- neonstatus.com reported eu-central-1 fully operational throughout). **Fix**: the existing `withTransientRetry(fn, maxAttempts)` call already took an attempt-count override (added DR-221's follow-up) but this call site was still on the shared default of 4 attempts / ~1.5s total backoff -- bumped to 10 attempts / ~7s total budget, same backoff shape, explicitly only for this one call site (user creation is a rare, admin-only action, not a hot path, so several extra seconds of patience here is cheap insurance against a slower-than-usual Neon pooler catch-up; no other `withTransientRetry` caller was touched). Live reproduction from this session's own sandbox was not possible (a separate, unrelated sandbox-to-Neon connectivity failure made every direct DB script time out during this investigation, confirmed not a Neon-side incident) -- this fix is based on production log evidence plus the ruled-out list above, not a locally-reproduced-then-verified fix; flagged to the user to retry in production and confirm. No schema change, no new permission, no module-dependency change. | V6, V8 |
| DR-227 | 2026-09-03 | **CI red on `main` since DR-222, unrelated to DR-223 through DR-226** -- `tests/api/booking-setup.api.test.ts`'s DR-222 (FLIGHT_TICKET/ESIM) `beforeAll` threw `PrismaClientKnownRequestError P2000` ("value too long for column") on `tx.booking.create()`, failing the whole suite before a single test in that block ran.
